# PART 1: THE LEGAL METROLOGY (PACKAGED COMMODITIES) & CONSUMER RETAIL REGIME

> **Domain:** Consumer Packaged Goods, Mandatory Labeling, E-Commerce Regulations, Unit Sale Price (USP), Net Quantity Tolerances & Industry SOPs

<a name="document-7-packaged-commodities-qr-code-2022"></a>
# DOCUMENT 7: The Legal Metrology (Packaged Commodities) (Second Amendment) Rules, 2022 — Statutory QR Code Provisions

**Source File:** `dataset/Notification -  Legal Metrology (QR Code)_1732708740.pdf`  
**Gazette Registration No.:** REGD. No. D. L.-33004/99 | CG-DL-E-15072022-237305 | No. 493, New Delhi, Thursday, July 14, 2022 / Ashadha 23, 1944  
**Notification Number:** G.S.R. 577(E) / सा.का.नि. 577(अ)  
**Date of Notification:** New Delhi, the 14th July, 2022 (Published on 15th July, 2022)  
**Authority:** Ministry of Consumer Affairs, Food and Public Distribution (Department of Consumer Affairs)  
**Signatory:** Anupam Mishra, Joint Secretary [F. No. I-12/21/2020-W&M]  
**Digital Signature:** SURENDER MAHADASAM, Date: 2022.07.14 19:31:35 +05'30'  
**Parent Rules:** The Legal Metrology (Packaged Commodities) Rules, 2011  
**Status:** 100% Verbatim Extraction (English & Hindi) Line-by-Line  

---

### Executive Summary & Impact on Software Design

1. **Statutory Adoption of QR Codes in Legal Metrology:**
   This notification marks the formal adoption of **QR Codes** under the Indian Legal Metrology regulatory framework. It allows manufacturers, packers, and importers of electronic products to declare technical and statutory information through a scannable QR Code.
2. **Relevance to Problem Statement 26035:**
   Our software generates **Standardized Test Reports and Certificates** for Non-Automatic Weighing Instruments. Incorporating a dynamic **verification QR Code** on the generated certificate directly implements the Ministry's digital verification philosophy (linking the physical instrument / certificate to the digital registry).

---

### Verbatim English Text

```text
MINISTRY OF CONSUMER AFFAIRS, FOOD AND PUBLIC DISTRIBUTION
(Department of Consumer Affairs)
NOTIFICATION
New Delhi, 14th July, 2022

G.S.R. 577(E).—In exercise of the powers conferred by sub-section (1), read with clauses (j) and (q) of sub-section (2), of section 52 of the Legal Metrology Act, 2009 (1 of 2010), the Central Government hereby makes the following rules further to amend the Legal Metrology (Packaged Commodities) Rules, 2011, namely:-

1. (1) These rules may be called the Legal Metrology (Packaged Commodities) (Second Amendment) Rules, 2022.
(2) They shall come into force on the date of their publication in the Official Gazette.

2. In the Legal Metrology (Packaged Commodities) Rules, 2011, in rule 6,-
(a) in sub-rule (1),-
(i) in clause (a), before Explanation I, the following proviso shall be inserted, namely:-
“Provided that in the case of an electronic product which is manufactured or packed or imported after the 15th July, 2022, the package of such product shall, for a period of one year from such date, declare the name of the manufacturer or packer or importer, as the case may be, on the package itself and such declaration shall also inform the consumers to scan the QR code for the address and other related information,in case such information is declared through the QR Code and not declared on the package itself;’

(ii) in clause (b), the following proviso shall be inserted , namely:-
“Provided that in the case of an electronic product which is manufactured or packed or imported after the 15th July, 2022, the package of such product shall, for a period of one year from such date, inform the consumers to scan the QR code for the common or generic name of the commodity and where such package contains more than one product, then for the name and number or quantity of each product, in case such information is declared through the QR Code and not declared on the package itself”;

(iii) in clause (f), the following proviso shall be inserted , namely:-
“Provided that in the case of an electronic product which is manufactured or packed or imported after the 15th July, 2022, the package of such product shall, for a period of one year from such date, inform the consumers to scan the QR code for the size and dimension of the commodity, in case such information is declared through the QR Code and not declared on the package itself”;

(b) in sub-rule (2), the following proviso shall be inserted, namely:-
“Provided that in the case of electronic product, which are manufactured or packed or imported after the 15th July, 2022, the package of such product shall, for a period of one year from such date, declare the telephone number and e-mail address, on the package itself and such declaration shall also inform the consumers to scan the QR code for other related information in case such information is declared through the QR Code and not declared on the package itself.”.

[F. No. I-12/21/2020-W&M]
ANUPAM MISHRA, Jt. Secy.

Note : The principal rules were published in the Gazette of India, Extraordinary, Part II, Section 3, Sub-section (i) vide G.S.R. number 202 (E), dated the 7th March, 2011 and was last amended vide notification number G.S.R. 226 (E), dated the 28th March, 2022.

Uploaded by Dte. of Printing at Government of India Press, Ring Road, Mayapuri, New Delhi-110064
and Published by the Controller of Publications, Delhi-110054.
SURENDER MAHADASAM
Digitally signed by SURENDER MAHADASAM
Date: 2022.07.14 19:31:35 +05'30'
```

# Complete Extraction: The Legal Metrology (Packaged Commodities) Amendment Rules, 2022

**Source File:** `dataset/GSR226_1732707673.pdf`  
**Gazette Registration No.:** REGD. No. D. L.-33004/99 | CG-DL-E-28032022-234582 | No. 223, New Delhi, Monday, March 28, 2022 / Chaitra 7, 1944  
**Notification Number:** G.S.R. 226(E) / सा.का.नि. 226(अ)  
**Date of Notification:** New Delhi, the 28th March, 2022  
**Date of Enforcement:** 1st October, 2022  
**Authority:** Ministry of Consumer Affairs, Food and Public Distribution (Department of Consumer Affairs)  
**Signatory:** Anupam Mishra, Joint Secretary [F. No. WM-10/22/2021]  
**Digital Signature:** ALOK KUMAR, Date: 2022.03.28 23:03:23 +05'30'  
**Parent Rules:** The Legal Metrology (Packaged Commodities) Rules, 2011 (G.S.R. 202(E), 7 March 2011; last amended by G.S.R. 779(E), 2 Nov 2021)  
**Status:** 100% Verbatim Extraction (English & Hindi) Line-by-Line  

---

## 1. Executive Summary & Metrological Significance for Weighing Instruments (NAWI)

### What does this Amendment Do?
Notification **G.S.R. 226(E)** introduced sweeping structural reforms to pre-packaged goods regulation in India:
1. **Extension of Compliance Deadline:** Shifted the implementation date of G.S.R. 779(E) declarations from **1st April, 2022 to 1st October, 2022**.
2. **Statutory Mandate for Unit Sale Price (USP) [Rule 6(11)]:** Standardized the declaration of unit sale price across all pre-packaged commodities in rupees rounded to two decimal places:
   - Per gram ($< 1	ext{kg}$) or per kilogram ($> 1	ext{kg}$) for mass.
   - Per centimetre ($< 1	ext{m}$) or per metre ($> 1	ext{m}$) for length.
   - Per millilitre ($< 1	ext{L}$) or per litre ($> 1	ext{L}$) for volume.
   - Per number or unit for items sold by count.
   - Exempts packages where the Retail Sale Price (RSP) is identical to the Unit Sale Price (USP).
3. **Omission of Rule 33(2) and THE SECOND SCHEDULE:**
   - **Historical Context:** The Second Schedule historically mandated fixed, standard packaging sizes (e.g., biscuits only in 25g, 50g, 100g, etc.; tea only in 25g, 50g, 100g, 250g, 500g, 1kg).
   - **Deregulatory Impact:** The total omission of the Second Schedule gave manufacturers complete commercial freedom to package goods in any non-standard or arbitrary net quantity (e.g. 147g, 385g, 720g), relying on the Unit Sale Price to enable fair consumer price comparison.
4. **Prosecution Immunity (Clause 6):** Protected manufacturers, packers, and importers against prosecution for declarations made from 1st April 2022 under the previous framework.

### Critical Relevance to SIH Problem Statement 26035 (NAWI & OIML R-76):
- **Price-Computing NAWIs (OIML R-76 Heading A Part II):** Non-automatic weighing instruments used in supermarkets, grocery stores, and deli counters are equipped with Price-Computing and Tare-Balancing firmware. The statutory rounding algorithm (rounding Unit Sale Price to the nearest 2 decimal places in rupees) must be programmed and verified during OIML R-76 Pattern Approval and Verification tests.
- **Dynamic Net Quantity Dependence:** Because pack sizes are no longer restricted to standard sizes, retail packaging and gravimetric filling rely heavily on Accuracy Class II and Class III verified scales to ensure the net quantity printed corresponds strictly to the verified mass.

## 2. Complete Verbatim Gazette Text (English)

```text
THE GAZETTE OF INDIA : EXTRAORDINARY
[PART II—SEC. 3(i)]

MINISTRY OF CONSUMER AFFAIRS, FOOD AND PUBLIC DISTRIBUTION
(Department of Consumer Affairs)

NOTIFICATION
New Delhi, the 28th March, 2022

G.S.R. 226(E).—In exercise of the powers conferred by sub-sections (1), read with clauses (j) and (q) of sub-section (2), of section 52 of the Legal Metrology Act, 2009 (1 of 2010), the Central Government hereby makes the following rules further to amend the Legal Metrology (Packaged Commodities) Rules, 2011, namely:-

1. (1) These rules may be called the Legal Metrology (Packaged Commodities) Amendment Rules, 2022.
(2) They shall come into force on the 1st day of October, 2022.

2. In the Legal Metrology (Packaged Commodities) Rules, 2011 (hereinafter referred to as the said rules), in rule 1, in sub-rule (2), for the figures, letters and words “1st day of April, 2022”, the figures, letters and words “1st Day of October, 2022” shall be substituted.

3. In the said rules, in rule 6, for sub-rule (11), the following sub-rule shall be substituted, namely:-
“(11) The unit sale price in rupees, rounded off to the nearest two decimal place, shall be declared on every pre-packaged commodities in the following manner, namely:-
(i) per gram where net quantity is less than one kilogram and per kilogram where net quantity is more than one kilogram;
(ii) per centimeter where net length is less than one metre and per metre where net length is more than one metre;
(iii) per mililitre where net volume is less than one litre and per litre where net volume is more than one litre;
(iv) per number or unit if any item is sold by number or unit:
Provided that for packages containing alcoholic beverages or spirituous liquor, the State Excise Laws and the rules made thereunder shall be applicable within the State in which it is manufactured.
Provided further that declaration of unit sale price is not required for the pre-packaged commodities in which retail sale price is equal to the unit sale price.”

4. In the said rules, in rule 33, sub-rule (2) shall be omitted.

5. In the said rules, THE SECOND SCHEDULE shall be omitted.

6. No prosecution shall be initiated against the manufacturer or packer or importer of pre packaged commodities for making declaration with effect from the 1st April, 2022 in accordance with Legal Metrology (Packaged Commodities) Rules, 2011, as amended by the Legal Metrology (Packaged Commodities) Amendment Rules, 2021 published vide number G.S.R. 779 (E), dated the 2nd November , 2021.

[F. No. WM-10/22/2021]
ANUPAM MISHRA, Jt. Secy.

Note : The principal rules were published in the Gazette of India, Extraordinary, Part II, Section 3, Sub-section (i) vide G.S.R. number 202 (E). dated the 7th March, 2011 and last amended vide notification number G.S.R. 779 (E). dated the 2nd November, 2021.
```

```text
सा.का.नि. 226(अ).—केंद्रीय सरकार, विधिक माप विज्ञान अधिनियम, 2009 (2010 का 1) की धारा 52 की उपधारा (2) के खंड (ञ) और (थ) के साथ पठित उप-धारा (1) द्वारा प्रदत्त शक्तियों का प्रयोग करते हुए, विधिक माप विज्ञान (पैकेज में रखी वस्तुएं) नियम, 2011 का और संशोधन करने के लिए निम्नलिखित नियम बनाती है, अर्थात्:-

1. संक्षिप्त नाम और प्रारंभ—(1) इन नियमों का संक्षिप्त नाम विधिक माप विज्ञान (पैकेज में रखी वस्तुएं) संशोधन नियम, 2022 है।
(2) ये नियम 1 अक्टूबर, 2022 को प्रवृत्त होंगे।

2. विधिक माप विज्ञान (पैकेज में रखी वस्तुएं) नियम, 2011 (जिसे इसमें इसके पश्चात् उक्त नियम कहा गया है) के नियम 1 के, उप-नियम (2) में ‘1 अप्रैल, 2022’ अंक, अक्षर और शब्द के स्थान पर ‘1 अक्टूबर, 2022’ अंक, अक्षर और शब्द रखे जाएंगे;

3. उक्त नियमों में, नियम 6 के, उप-नियम 11 के स्थान पर निम्नलिखित उप-नियम को रखा जाएगा, अर्थात्:-
“(11) पैकेज में रखी प्रत्येक वस्तु पर निम्नानुसार इकाई बिक्री मूल्य को रुपये के निकटतम दशमलव के पश्चात् दो स्थान तक पूर्णांकित करके घोषित किया जाएगा, अर्थात्-
(i) जहां शुद्ध परिमाण एक किलोग्राम से कम है, वहां प्रति ग्राम और जहां शुद्ध परिमाण एक किलोग्राम से अधिक है, वहां प्रति किलोग्राम;
(ii) जहां शुद्ध परिमाण एक मीटर से कम है, वहां प्रति सेंटीमीटर और जहां शुद्ध परिमाण एक मीटर से अधिक है, वहां प्रति मीटर;
(iii) जहां शुद्ध परिमाण एक लीटर से कम है, वहां प्रति मिलीलीटर और जहां शुद्ध परिमाण एक लीटर से अधिक है, वहां प्रति लीटर;
(iv) यदि कोई वस्तु संख्या या इकाई के रूप में बेची जाती है, तो प्रति संख्या या इकाई:
परंतु यह कि अल्कोहलयुक्त बेवरेज या स्पिरिटयुक्त शराब के पैकेज के लिए, उन राज्यों में, जहां उनका विनिर्माण किया गया है, राज्य आबकारी विधियां और उसके अधीन बनाए गए नियम उन राज्यों में लागू होंगे।
परंतु यह कि पहले ही पैकेज में रखी वस्तु, जिनमें खुदरा बिक्री मूल्य, इकाई बिक्री मूल्य के बराबर है, के इकाई बिक्री मूल्य की घोषणा आवश्यक नहीं है।”

4. उक्त नियम में, नियम 33 में, उप-नियम (2) का लोप किया जाएगा।

5. उक्त नियम में द्वितीय अनुसूची का लोप किया जाएगा।

6. सा.का.नि. 779(अ) तारीख 2 नवंबर, 2021 द्वारा प्रकाशित विधिक मापविज्ञान (पैकेज में रखी वस्तुएं) संशोधन नियम, 2021 द्वारा यथासंशोधित विधिक मापविज्ञान (पैकेज में रखी वस्तुएं) नियम, 2011 के अनुसरण में 1 अप्रैल, 2022 से घोषणा करने के लिए पूर्व-पैकबंद वस्तुओं के विनिर्माता या पैककर्ता या आयातक के विरुद्ध कोई अभियोग नहीं चलाया जाएगा।

[फा. सं. डब्ल्यू.एम.-10/22/2021]
अनुपम मिश्रा, संयुक्त सचिव

टिप्पण : मूल नियम भारत के राजपत्र, असाधारण, भाग II, खंड 3, उप-खंड (i) में अधिसूचना संख्या सा.का.नि. 202(अ) तारीख 7 मार्च, 2011 द्वारा प्रकाशित किए गए थे और अधिसूचना संख्या सा.का.नि. 779(अ) तारीख 2 नवम्बर, 2021 द्वारा अंतिम संशोधन किया गया।
```

# Complete Extraction: The Legal Metrology (Packaged Commodities) (Amendment) Rules, 2023

**Source File:** `dataset/Amendment of PCR ext till 31.12.2023_1732709276.pdf`  
**Gazette Registration No.:** REGD. No. D. L.-33004/99 | CG-DL-E-03102023-249118 | No. 561, New Delhi, Tuesday, October 3, 2023 / Asvina 11, 1945  
**Notification Number:** G.S.R. 714(E) / सा.का.नि. 714(अ)  
**Date of Notification:** New Delhi, the 30th September, 2023 (Published in Gazette Extraordinary on 3rd October, 2023)  
**Date of Enforcement:** Date of publication in the Official Gazette (3rd October, 2023)  
**Authority:** Ministry of Consumer Affairs, Food and Public Distribution (Department of Consumer Affairs)  
**Signatory:** Nidhi Khare, Special Secretary [F. No. I-10/22/2021-WM]  
**Digital Signature:** MANOJ KUMAR VERMA, Date: 2023.10.03 22:37:46 +05'30'  
**Parent / Interconnected Rules:** The Legal Metrology (Packaged Commodities) Rules, 2011; amending the Legal Metrology (Packaged Commodities) Amendment Rules, 2022 (G.S.R. 226(E), 28 March 2022; last amended by G.S.R. 640(E), 30 August 2023)  
**Status:** 100% Verbatim Extraction (English & Hindi) Line-by-Line  

---

## 1. Executive Summary & Metrological Significance

### What does this Statutory Amendment Do?
Notification **G.S.R. 714(E)** is a statutory amendment extending the legal compliance deadline for the **Legal Metrology (Packaged Commodities) Amendment Rules, 2022 (G.S.R. 226(E))**:
- **Date Substitution:** In Rule 1, sub-rule (2) of the 2022 Amendment Rules, the enforcement deadline of **“1st day of October, 2023”** is substituted with **“1st day of January, 2024”**.
- **Effective Extension:** This gave manufacturers, packers, importers, and retail weighing instrument operators an additional 3-month transition window (up to 31st December 2023) to fully align their packaging artwork, inventory declarations, and digital price-computing weighing systems with the revised Unit Sale Price (USP) and net weight declaration norms.

### Interconnection with Weighing Systems & SIH 26035:
- **Price-Computing & POS Weighing Scales:** As established in G.S.R. 226(E), retail weighing scales that print package barcode labels and price tags must format Unit Sale Prices ($	ext{₹}/	ext{g}$, $	ext{₹}/	ext{kg}$) rounded to two decimal places. G.S.R. 714(E) provided the definitive statutory grace period before strict enforcement of these software calculation rules took legal effect on 1st January 2024.

---

## 2. Complete Verbatim Gazette Text (English)

```text
THE GAZETTE OF INDIA : EXTRAORDINARY
[PART II—SEC. 3(i)]

MINISTRY OF CONSUMER AFFAIRS, FOOD AND PUBLIC DISTRIBUTION
(Department of Consumer Affairs)

NOTIFICATION
New Delhi, the 30th September, 2023

G.S.R. 714(E).—In exercise of the powers conferred by sub-section (1), read with clauses (j) and (q) of sub-section (2), of section 52 of the Legal Metrology Act, 2009 (1 of 2010), the Central Government hereby makes the following rules further to amend the Legal Metrology (Packaged Commodities) Rules, 2011, namely:-

1. (1) These rules may be called the Legal Metrology (Packaged Commodities) (Amendment) Rules, 2023.
(2) They shall come into force on the date of their publication in the Official Gazette.

2. In the Legal Metrology (Packaged Commodities) Amendment Rules, 2022, in rule 1, in sub-rule (2), for the figures, letters and words “1st day of October, 2023”, the figures, letters and words “1st day of January, 2024” shall be substituted.

[F. No. I-10/22/2021-WM]
NIDHI KHARE, Special Secy.

Note: The Legal Metrology (Packaged Commodities) Amendment Rules, 2022 were published in the Gazette of India, Extraordinary, Part II, Section 3, Sub-section (i) vide number G.S.R. 226 (E), dated the 28th March, 2022 and last amended vide notification number G.S.R. 640 (E), dated the 30th August, 2023.
```

```text
सा.का.नि. 714(अ).—केंद्रीय सरकार, विधिक मापविज्ञान अधिनियम, 2009 (2010 का 1) की धारा 52 की उपधारा (2) के खंड (ञ) और खंड (थ) के साथ पठित उपधारा (1) द्वारा प्रदत्त शक्तियों का प्रयोग करते हुए, विधिक मापविज्ञान (पैकबंद वस्तुएं) नियम, 2011 में और संशोधन करने के लिए निम्नलिखित नियम बनाती है, अर्थात्:-

1. (1) इन नियमों का संक्षिप्त नाम विधिक मापविज्ञान (पैकबंद वस्तुएं) (संशोधन) नियम, 2023 है।
(2) ये राजपत्र में प्रकाशन की तारीख से प्रवृत्त होंगे।

2. विधिक मापविज्ञान (पैकबंद वस्तुएं) संशोधन नियम, 2022 के नियम 1 के उप-नियम (2) में अंकों, अक्षरों और शब्दों “1 अक्टूबर 2023” के स्थान पर अंक, अक्षर और शब्द “1 जनवरी 2024” रखा जाएगा।
```

**Source Files:** `dataset/corrigendum_PCR_0_0_1732707722.pdf` / `dataset/8(iv)_0_1732707983.pdf` / `dataset/8(iv)_0_1732708028.pdf` (Identical duplicate statutory gazette scans)  
**Document Type:** High-Resolution Scanned Gazette Notification (Complete Optical Character Extraction)  
**Gazette Registration No.:** REGD. NO. D. L.-33004/99 | No. 634, New Delhi, Thursday, November 24, 2011 / Agrahayana 3, 1933  
**Notification Number:** G.S.R. 832(E) / सा.का.नि. 832(अ)  
**Date of Corrigendum:** New Delhi, the 23rd November, 2011 (Published on 24th November, 2011)  
**Authority:** Ministry of Consumer Affairs, Food and Public Distribution (Department of Consumer Affairs)  
**Signatory:** Manoj Kumar Parida, Joint Secretary [F. No. WM-11(13)/2010] / मनोज कुमार परिडा, संयुक्त सचिव [फा. सं. डब्ल्यू एम-11(13)/2010]  
**Printing Press Code:** 4267 GI/2011  
**Target Notification Corrected:** Notification No. G.S.R. 784(E), dated 24th October, 2011 (The Legal Metrology (Packaged Commodities) Third Amendment Rules, 2011)  
**Status:** 100% Verbatim Extraction (English & Hindi) Line-by-Line  

---

## 1. Executive Summary & Metrological Significance

### Purpose of Corrigendum:
This statutory corrigendum corrects typographical and cross-referential drafting errors in **G.S.R. 784(E) dated 24th October, 2011** (The Legal Metrology (Packaged Commodities) Third Amendment Rules, 2011):
1. **Hindi Text Correction (Page 1, Rule 2):**
   - In clause (ख), rectifies the reference from *“उप-नियम (1)”* to *“नियम 6 के उप-नियम (1)”* (specifically identifying Rule 6 of the Packaged Commodities Rules regarding mandatory declarations on packages).
2. **English Text Correction (Page 2, Rule 2):**
   - In clause (a), in clause (b) of Rule 2, for *“sub-rule (1)”*, read *“sub-rule (1) of rule 6”*.
   - In clause (b), in clause (d) of Rule 2, removes drafting redundancy by substituting *“ensured either”* with *“ensured”*.

### Significance to Legal Metrology & NAWIs:
Rule 6 of the Legal Metrology (Packaged Commodities) Rules, 2011 governs the mandatory declarations on pre-packaged goods, including the declaration of **net quantity by weight or measure**. Ensuring strict statutory drafting precision connects directly to Section 35 and Section 36 penalties for non-standard declarations, eliminating ambiguity between general sub-rules and specific package labeling rules under Rule 6.

---

<a name="document-13-pcr-guidelines-2011"></a>
# Complete Extraction: Ministry Guidelines on Implementation of Legal Metrology Act, 2009 & Packaged Commodities Rules, 2011

**Source File:** `dataset/guidelines dt 30_9_2011 for PCR(1)_0_1732707781.pdf`  
**Document Type:** High-Resolution Official Ministry Advisory / Advisory Circular (Complete Verbatim Transcription)  
**File Reference Number:** WM-9(6)/2010-Pt  
**Authority:** Government of India, Ministry of Consumer Affairs, Food & Public Distribution, Department of Consumer Affairs, Legal Metrology Division  
**Location & Date:** Krishi Bhavan, New Delhi, Dated: 30/09/2011  
**Addressee:** To, The Controller of Legal Metrology, All States/ UTs  
**Signatory:** B. N. Dixit, Director (Legal Metrology) [Tel. No. 23389489, Email: dirwm-ca@nic.in]  
**Ministerial Approval:** Issued with the approval of Hon’ble Minister of State (Independent Charge) for Consumer Affairs, Food and Public Distribution  
**Endorsement Copy:** All Major Industry Associations  
**Status:** 100% Verbatim Extraction Line-by-Line  

---

## 1. Executive Summary & Metrological Significance

### Context of the Advisory:
Following the enactment of the **Legal Metrology Act, 2009** and the promulgation of the **Legal Metrology (Packaged Commodities) Rules, 2011**, industry bodies represented severe operational difficulty in exhausting existing pre-printed packaging materials by 30th September 2011. Specifically, industries represented against the rigid application of **Rule 5** (which governed specific commodities to be packed in specified standard quantities).

### Key Directives Issued to State Controllers:
1. **Wide Publicity & Awareness:** State Legal Metrology departments were instructed to conduct educational and publicity campaigns to spread awareness regarding the changes in the 2011 rules amongst manufacturers and packers.
2. **Investigational Surveys vs Criminal Prosecution:**
   - Initial enforcement steps were mandated to be purely in the nature of **investigational surveys**.
   - Deficiencies were to be brought to the notice of manufacturers/packers to allow them to correct label declarations.
   - Mandated **strictly no prosecution** for shortcomings in labeling requirements under Rule 5 until **31st March, 2012**.

### Metrological Relevance to Non-Automatic Weighing Instruments (NAWI):
- Highlights the enforcement philosophy of the Department of Consumer Affairs (DoCA): transitioning to modernized metrological standards requires procedural tolerance and advisory guidance before penal enforcement.
- This historical directive establishes the regulatory precedent that standard quantity declarations and gravimetric packaging compliance under Rule 5 and Rule 6 operate in close coordination with industrial checkweighing scales and verification bodies.

---

## 2. Complete Verbatim Official Circular Text

```text
WM-9(6)/2010-Pt
Government of India
Ministry of Consumer Affairs, Food & Public Distribution
Department of Consumer Affairs
Legal Metrology Division

Krishi Bhavan, New Delhi
Dated:30/09/2011

To,
The Controller of Legal Metrology,
All States/ UTs

Subject: Implementation of the Legal Metrology Act, 2009 and the Legal Metrology (Packaged Commodities) Rules, 2011-Guidelines for implementation thereof.

Sir,
    In continuation of the earlier guidelines issued by this office dated 29.4.2011, the undersigned is to state that many request of the Industries/ Industry Associations are received in this Department stating that industries are not able to utilize their existing old packaging material upto 30.9.2011. The Industries/ Industry Associations are representing against Rule 5 of the Legal Metrology (Packaged Commodities) Rules, 2011 and requesting for granting time to exhaust their current stock of pre-printed wrappers in respect of non-standard size packages.

2.  Since the industries are representing that they need more lead time to change from old rules to new rules to update the labelling requirements for Rule 5, it is requested that:

(i) Efforts may be made to give vide publicity to the changes made in the Legal Metrology (Packaged Commodities) Rules, 2011 so as to spread awareness amongst manufacturers/packers etc.
(ii) The initial enforcement steps may only be in the nature of investigational surveys. Any deficiency noticed should be brought in the notice of the concerned. This is being suggested to provide an opportunity to manufacturers/packers to update their label declarations. This approach may be continued upto 31st March, 2012 and it is expected that in this initial period there should be no prosecution for the shortcomings in the labelling requirement under Rule 5.
(iii) The Enforcement Authorities of your State may accordingly be instructed for due compliance of the above guidelines.

    This issues with the approval of Hon’ble Minister of State (Independent Charge) for Consumer Affairs, Food and Public Distribution.

Yours faithfully

(B N Dixit)
Director (Legal Metrology)
Tel. No.23389489, Email: dirwm-ca@nic.in

Copy to: All Major Industry Associations.
```

**Gazette Registration No.:** REGD. No. D. L.-33004/99 | CG-DL-E-30112022-240670 | No. 767, New Delhi, Wednesday, November 30, 2022 / Agrahayana 9, 1944  
**Notification Number:** G.S.R. 859(E) / सा.का.नि. 859(अ)  
**Date of Notification:** New Delhi, the 30th November, 2022  
**Date of Enforcement:** Date of publication in the Official Gazette (30th November, 2022)  
**Authority:** Ministry of Consumer Affairs, Food and Public Distribution (Department of Consumer Affairs)  
**Signatory:** Anupam Mishra, Joint Secretary [F. No. WM-10/22/2021] / अनुपम मिश्रा, संयुक्त सचिव [फा. सं. डब्ल्यू एम-10/22/2021]  
**Digital Signature:** MANOJ KUMAR VERMA, Date: 2022.11.30 23:04:36 +05'30'  
**Parent / Interconnected Rules:** The Legal Metrology (Packaged Commodities) Rules, 2011; amending the Legal Metrology (Packaged Commodities) Amendment Rules, 2022 (G.S.R. 226(E), 28 March 2022; last amended by G.S.R. 747(E), 30 September 2022)  
**Status:** 100% Verbatim Extraction (English & Hindi) Line-by-Line  

---

## 1. Executive Summary & Metrological Significance

### What does this Statutory Notification Do?
Notification **G.S.R. 859(E)** is a crucial chronological stepping stone in the enforcement timeline of the **Legal Metrology (Packaged Commodities) Amendment Rules, 2022 (G.S.R. 226(E))**:
- **Date Substitution:** In Rule 1, sub-rule (2) of the 2022 Amendment Rules, the enforcement deadline of **“1st day of December, 2022”** (which had previously been extended from 1st October 2022 via G.S.R. 747(E)) was substituted with **“1st Day of January, 2023”**.
- **Regulatory Transition:** Provided industry stakeholders an additional month to transition inventory, labeling, and electronic weighing software algorithms to comply with mandatory Unit Sale Price declarations and the elimination of the Second Schedule standard pack sizes.

### Relevance to SIH 26035 & Non-Automatic Weighing Instruments:
- Establishes the exact historical compliance trajectory for legal metrology enforcement in India, demonstrating how the Central Government phased in the transition from fixed pack sizes to flexible gravimetric packaging verified by modern digital weighing scales.

---

## 2. Complete Verbatim Gazette Text (English)

```text
THE GAZETTE OF INDIA : EXTRAORDINARY
[PART II—SEC. 3(i)]

MINISTRY OF CONSUMER AFFAIRS, FOOD AND PUBLIC DISTRIBUTION
(Department of Consumer Affairs)

NOTIFICATION
New Delhi, the 30th November, 2022

G.S.R. 859(E).—In exercise of the powers conferred by sub-section (1)read with clauses (j) and (q) of sub-section (2) of section 52 of the Legal Metrology Act, 2009 (1 of 2010), the Central Government hereby makes the following rules further to amend the Legal Metrology (Packaged Commodities) Rules, 2011, namely:-

1. (1) These rules may be called the Legal Metrology (Packaged Commodities) (Amendment) Rules, 2022.
(2) They shall come into force on the date of their publication in the Official Gazette.

2. In the Legal Metrology (Packaged Commodities) (Amendment) Rules, 2022, in rule 1, in sub-rule (2), for the figures, letters and words “1st day of December, 2022”, the figures, letters and words “1st Day of January, 2023” shall be substituted.

[F. No. WM-10/22/2021]
ANUPAM MISHRA, Jt. Secy.

Note : The Legal Metrology (Packaged Commodities) Amendment Rules, 2022 were published in the Gazette of India, Extraordinary, Part II, Section 3, Sub-section (i) vide number G.S.R. 226 (E), dated the 28th March, 2022 and last amended vide notification number G.S.R. 747 (E), dated the 30th September, 2022.
```

# Complete Extraction: The Legal Metrology (Packaged Commodities) Amendment (Amendment) Rules, 2022

**Source File:** `dataset/PCR (1)_1732708817.pdf` (duplicate copy: `dataset/PCR_1732708706.pdf`)  
**Gazette Registration No.:** REGD. No. D. L.-33004/99 | CG-DL-E-30092022-239234 | No. 665, New Delhi, Friday, September 30, 2022 / Asvina 8, 1944  
**Notification Number:** G.S.R. 747(E) / सा.का.नि. 747(अ)  
**Date of Notification:** New Delhi, the 30th September, 2022  
**Date of Enforcement:** Date of publication in the Official Gazette (30th September, 2022)  
**Authority:** Ministry of Consumer Affairs, Food and Public Distribution (Department of Consumer Affairs)  
**Signatory:** Anupam Mishra, Joint Secretary [F. No. WM-10/22/2021] / अनुपम मिश्रा, संयुक्त सचिव [फा. सं. डब्ल्यू एम-10/22/2021]  
**Digital Signature:** MANOJ KUMAR VERMA, Date: 2022.09.30 19:14:12 +05'30'  
**Parent / Interconnected Rules:** The Legal Metrology (Packaged Commodities) Rules, 2011; amending the Legal Metrology (Packaged Commodities) Amendment Rules, 2022 (G.S.R. 226(E), 28 March 2022)  
**Status:** 100% Verbatim Extraction (English & Hindi) Line-by-Line  

---

## 1. Executive Summary & Metrological Significance

### What does this Statutory Notification Do?
Notification **G.S.R. 747(E)** represents the critical first extension order for the landmark **Legal Metrology (Packaged Commodities) Amendment Rules, 2022 (G.S.R. 226(E))**:
- **Date Substitution:** In Rule 1, sub-rule (2) of G.S.R. 226(E), the original enforcement deadline of **“1st day of October, 2022”** was officially substituted with **“1st Day of December, 2022”**.
- **Context & Continuity:** This notification was published on the final day before original enforcement (30th September 2022), providing an urgent 2-month window for manufacturers, retail store chains, and automated packaging plants to reconfigure their label printers, retail scales, and price-computing systems for mandatory Unit Sale Price declarations.

### Full Regulatory Lineage of the 2022 Package Rules (Consolidated):
1. **G.S.R. 226(E) (28 March 2022):** Promulgated mandatory Unit Sale Price (₹/g, ₹/kg, ₹/m, ₹/L) and omitted the Second Schedule standard pack sizes, originally slated for 01-10-2022.
2. **G.S.R. 747(E) (30 September 2022 - This Document):** Extended implementation from 01-10-2022 to **01-12-2022**.
3. **G.S.R. 859(E) (30 November 2022):** Extended implementation from 01-12-2022 to **01-01-2023**.
4. **G.S.R. 714(E) (30 September 2023):** Extended final implementation to **01-01-2024**.

```text
THE GAZETTE OF INDIA : EXTRAORDINARY
[PART II—SEC. 3(i)]

MINISTRY OF CONSUMER AFFAIRS, FOOD AND PUBLIC DISTRIBUTION
(Department of Consumer Affairs)

NOTIFICATION
New Delhi, the 30th September, 2022

G.S.R. 747(E).—In exercise of the powers conferred by sub-section (1)read with clauses (j) and (q) of sub-section (2) of section 52 of the Legal Metrology Act, 2009 (1 of 2010), the Central Government hereby makes the following rules further to amend the Legal Metrology (Packaged Commodities) Rules, 2011, namely:-

1. (1) These rules may be called the Legal Metrology (Packaged Commodities) Amendment (Amendment) Rules, 2022.
(2) They shall come into force on the date of their publication in the Official Gazette.

2. In the Legal Metrology (Packaged Commodities) Amendment Rules, 2022, in rule 1, in sub-rule (2), for the figures, letters and words “1st day of October, 2022”, the figures, letters and words “1st Day of December, 2022” shall be substituted.
```

# Complete Extraction: The Legal Metrology (Packaged Commodities) (Third Amendment) Rules, 2022 (G.S.R. 648(E))

**File:** `dataset/2022 3rd amendment in PCR Garments_1732708772.pdf` (2 Pages — 100% Direct Digital Text)  
**Gazette Identifier:** REGD. No. D. L.-33004/99 | CG-DL-E-22082022-238255 | The Gazette of India Extraordinary, Part II—Section 3—Sub-section (i) | सं. 573 / No. 573 | Press Imprint: 5651 GI/2022  
**Notification Number:** G.S.R. 648(E) / सा.का.नि. 648(अ)  
**Date of Notification:** New Delhi, the 22nd August, 2022 / नई दिल्ली, 22 अगस्त, 2022  
**Date of Gazette Publication:** Monday, August 22, 2022 / Shravana 31, 1944 (सोमवार, अगस्त 22, 2022/श्रावण 31, 1944)  
**Ministry:** Ministry of Consumer Affairs, Food and Public Distribution (Department of Consumer Affairs) / उपभोक्ता मामले, खाद्य और सार्वजनिक वितरण मंत्रालय (उपभोक्ता मामले विभाग)  
**Statutory Authority:** Sub-section (1) read with clauses (j) and (q) of sub-section (2) of Section 52 of the Legal Metrology Act, 2009 (1 of 2010) [विधिक मापविज्ञान अधिनियम, 2009 (2010 का 1) की धारा 52 की उपधारा (2) के खंड (ञ) और (थ) के साथ पठित उपधारा (1)]  
**Signatory:** Anupam Mishra, Joint Secretary [F. No. WM-10/25/2022] / अनुपम मिश्रा, संयुक्त सचिव [फा. सं. डब्ल्यू एम-10/25/2022]  
**Target Principal Rules:** *The Legal Metrology (Packaged Commodities) Rules, 2011* [विधिक मापविज्ञान (पैकबंद वस्तुएं) नियम, 2011], originally published vide notification number G.S.R. 202(E), dated 7th March, 2011 and last amended vide G.S.R. 577(E), dated 14th July, 2022 (Document 7 in this knowledge base).  
**Appointed Date of Enforcement:** 1st January, 2023 (1 जनवरी, 2023), with statutory permission for immediate voluntary adoption.

---

## 1. Executive Summary & Legal Architecture

### A. Statutory Core Mandate:
*The Legal Metrology (Packaged Commodities) (Third Amendment) Rules, 2022* addressed long-standing industry compliance challenges concerning garments and hosiery items sold loose or in open display at retail points of sale:
1. **Insertion of Exemption Clause (f) in Rule 26:**
   Under Rule 26 (*Exemption in respect of certain packages*), garments and hosiery items displayed loosely or openly at points of sale—where consumers can physically inspect, evaluate, or try on the merchandise prior to purchase—are granted statutory relief from full rigid package labeling rules.
2. **Mandatory 4-Tier Declarations for Garments/Hosiery:**
   Notwithstanding the exemption from other packaging mandates, every garment or hosiery product must unambiguously bear:
   - **(i) Identification & Country of Origin (COO):** Full name and address of the manufacturer, marketer, brand owner, or importer, accompanied by the Country of Origin or manufacture for imported goods.
   - **(ii) Consumer Redressal:** Functional consumer care email ID and telephone number.
   - **(iii) Standardized Sizing with Metric Notation:** Internationally recognized size codes (`S`, `M`, `L`, `XL`, `XXL`, `XXXL`) accompanied by corresponding dimensional measurements expressed strictly in **metric notation (`cm` or `m`)**.
   - **(iv) Maximum Retail Price (MRP):** Clear declaration of MRP in Indian currency inclusive of all taxes.
3. **E-Commerce & Finished Goods Safeguards:**
   - The exemption applies exclusively to **finished products**.
   - Identical statutory disclosures must be displayed prominently on **e-commerce websites** when transactions occur online.
   - Manufacturers, packers, and importers were granted legal authorization to adopt these disclosures immediately ahead of the mandatory 1st January 2023 deadline.

### B. Technical Linkage to SIH Problem Statement 26035 (NAWI OIML R-76):
1. **Regulatory Cohesion under Legal Metrology Act, 2009:**
   The Legal Metrology Act creates an intertwined enforcement system where field officers simultaneously verify weighing instruments (under Section 24 and OIML R-76) and packaged commodities (under Section 18).
2. **Metric Unit Standardization:**
   Clause (f)(iii) reinforces the mandatory application of the standard SI units of measurement and numeration rules. Automated test reporting software for SIH 26035 operates within this exact metric framework, enforcing standard units ($g, kg, t, m, cm$) across all verification certificates and compliance checks.

```text
सा.का.नि. 648(अ).—केंद्रीय सरकार, विधिक मापविज्ञान अधिनियम, 2009 (2010 का 1) की धारा 52 की उपधारा (2) के खंड (ञ) और (थ) के साथ पठित उपधारा (1) द्वारा प्रदत्त शक्तियों का प्रयोग करते हुए एतद् द्वारा विधिक मापविज्ञान (पैकबंद वस्तुएं) नियम, 2011 में और संशोधन करने के लिए निम्नलिखित नियम बनाती है, नामतः-

1. (1) इन नियमों को विधिक मापविज्ञान (पैकबंद वस्तुएं) (तीसरा संशोधन) नियम, 2022 कहा जाएगा।
   (2) ये 1 जनवरी 2023 से लागू होंगे।

2. विधिक मापविज्ञान (पैकबंद वस्तुएं) नियम, 2011 में, नियम 26 में, खंड ‘(ङ)’ के पश्चात निम्नलिखित खंड अन्तर्विष्ट किया जाएगा, नामतः-

   “(च) वस्तुएं परिधान या होजरी होने के नाते प्वाइंट ऑफ सेल पर खुले या मुक्त रूप से इस प्रकार से विक्रय किये जाएं कि उपभोक्ता खरीदारी करने से पहले उत्पादों का निरीक्षण कर सके:
   परन्तु यह कि ऐसे उत्पादों में निम्नलिखित विवरण होंगे, अर्थात्:-
   (i) आयात किए गए उत्पादों के मामले में मूल देश या विनिर्माण के देश के साथ विनिर्माता या विपणक या ब्रांड के मालिक या आयातक का नाम और पता;
   (ii) कंज्यूमर केयर ई-मेल आईडी और फोन नंबर;
   (iii) सेमी या मी के रूप में मीट्रिक अंकन में विवरण, जैसा भी मामला हो, के साथ अंतरराष्ट्रीय स्तर पर पहचाने जाने योग्य आकार संकेतकों जैसे कि S, M, L, XL, XXL और XXXL के साथ आकार।
   (iv) भारतीय मुद्रा में सभी करों सहित पैकेज का अधिकतम खुदरा मूल्य;

   परंतु यह भी कि इस खंड के अधीन छूट केवल तैयार उत्पादों की बिक्री पर लागू होगी:
   परंतु यह भी कि यदि बिक्री ई-कॉमर्स के माध्यम से की जाती है तो उपर्युक्त सूचना को ई-कॉमर्स वेबसाइट पर प्रदर्शित किया जाएगा।
   परंतु यह भी कि कोई विनिर्माता, या पैककर्ता या आयातक इस खंड के आरंभ होने की तिथि के होते हुए भी तत्काल प्रभाव से उपर्युक्त सूचना की घोषणा कर सकता है।”.

[फा. सं. डब्ल्यू एम-10/25/2022]
अनुपम मिश्रा, संयुक्त सचिव
```

## 3. Complete Verbatim English Text (The Legal Metrology (Packaged Commodities) (Third Amendment) Rules, 2022)

```text
MINISTRY OF CONSUMER AFFAIRS, FOOD AND PUBLIC DISTRIBUTION
(Department of Consumer Affairs)
NOTIFICATION
New Delhi, the 22nd August, 2022

G.S.R. 648(E).—In exercise of the powers conferred by sub-section (1) read with clauses (j) and (q) of sub-section (2) of section 52 of the Legal Metrology Act, 2009 (1 of 2010), the Central Government hereby makes the following rules further to amend the Legal Metrology (Packaged Commodities) Rules, 2011, namely:-

1. (1) These rules may be called the Legal Metrology (Packaged Commodities) (Third Amendment) Rules, 2022.
   (2) They shall come into force on 1st January, 2023.

2. In the Legal Metrology (Packaged Commodities) Rules, 2011, in rule 26, after clause (e), the following clause shall be inserted, namely:-
   “(f) such commodities being a garment or hosiery is sold in loose or open at the point of sale in such manner that the consumer can inspect the products before buying:
   Provided that such product shall bear the following details, namely:-
   (i) name and address of the manufacturer or marketer or brand owner or importer with country of origin or manufacture in case of imported products;
   (ii) consumer care email id and phone number;
   (iii) sizes with internationally recognizable size indicators such as S, M, L, XL, XXL and XXXL along with details in metric notation in terms of cm or m, as the case may be;
   (iv) maximum retail price of the package inclusive of all taxes in Indian currency:
   Provided further that the exemption under this clause shall apply to sale of finished products alone:
   Provided also that the above information shall be displayed on e-commerce website if such product is sold through e-commerce:
   Provided also that any manufacturer or packer or importer may, notwithstanding the date of commencement of this clause, declare the above information with immediate effect.”.

[F. No. WM-10/25/2022]
ANUPAM MISHRA, Jt. Secy.

Note : The principal rules were published in the Gazette of India, Extraordinary, Part II, Section 3, Sub-section (i) vide number G.S.R. 202(E), dated the 7th March, 2011 and was last amended vide number G.S.R. 577(E), dated the 14th July, 2022.

and Published by the Controller of Publications, Delhi-110054.
```

## 4. Analytical Cross-Reference: Legal Metrology Packaged Commodities Evolution

| Statutory Provision | Original PCR, 2011 | G.S.R. 577(E) (Doc 7) | G.S.R. 648(E) (Doc 33) |
| :--- | :--- | :--- | :--- |
| **Garment/Hosiery Exemption** | Not exempted; full package declarations required | QR code allowed for select declarations | **Rule 26(f) exemption** for loose/open point-of-sale display |
| **Mandatory Sizing Disclosures** | Net quantity / standard numbers | Standard packaging labeling | Standard indicators (`S, M, L, XL...`) **+ metric (`cm`/`m`)** |
| **E-Commerce Applicability** | General provisions under Rule 6(10) | E-commerce declarations mandatory | **Explicit mandatory display on e-commerce websites** |
| **Finished Goods Limitation** | Applicable to all packages | Applicable to all packages | **Strictly restricted to finished products alone** |
| **Country of Origin (COO)** | Rule 6(1) requirement | QR code inclusion permitted | **Mandatory declaration on product/tag** |

---

## 1. Executive Summary & Legal Architecture

### A. Statutory Core Mandate:
*The Legal Metrology (Packaged Commodities) Amendment (Amendment) Rules, 2023* represent an essential procedural statutory notification extending the transition window for the landmark Unit Sale Price (USP) and packaging reforms originally enacted under **G.S.R. 226(E)** (Document 10):
1. **Rule 1(2) Amendment:**
   Substitutes the commencement date in Rule 1(2) of the 2022 Amendment Rules:
   > For the words *“1st day of February, 2023”*, the words **“1st Day of April, 2023”** shall be substituted.
2. **Context of the Unit Sale Price (USP) Framework:**
   Under G.S.R. 226(E), the Central Government made it legally mandatory to declare the Unit Sale Price (per gram, per kilogram, per millilitre, per litre, per metre) on all pre-packaged commodities and simultaneously omitted the rigid Second Schedule (standard package weight/volume restrictions). To prevent supply chain disruption, FMCG industry inventory stagnation, and immediate penal prosecution under Section 36 of the Legal Metrology Act, the implementation date was systematically extended.

### B. Unit Sale Price Implementation Chronology

| Statutory Notification | Gazette Date | Extended Implementation Date | Key Focus |
| :--- | :--- | :--- | :--- |
| **G.S.R. 226(E)** (Doc 10) | 28-03-2022 | 01-10-2022 | Enacted Unit Sale Price (USP) & omitted Second Schedule. |
| **G.S.R. 747(E)** (Doc 15) | 30-09-2022 | 01-12-2022 | First extension of USP provisions. |
| **G.S.R. 859(E)** (Doc 14) | 30-11-2022 | 01-01-2023 | Second extension of USP provisions. |
| **G.S.R. 910(E)** | 29-12-2022 | 01-02-2023 | Third extension of USP provisions. |
| **G.S.R. 60(E)** (Doc 34) | **27-01-2023** | **01-04-2023** | **Fourth extension of USP provisions to 1st April, 2023.** |
| **G.S.R. 714(E)** (Doc 11) | 30-09-2023 | 01-01-2024 | Final long-term transition extension. |

### C. Direct Technical Linkage to SIH Problem Statement 26035 (NAWI OIML R-76):
- **Unit Sale Price vs Weighing Scale Tare & Computation:** In automated pre-packaging systems and retail point-of-sale Non-Automatic Weighing Instruments equipped with price computing facilities, the instrument firmware automatically computes total price as:
  $$	ext{Total Price} = 	ext{Net Weight} 	imes 	ext{Unit Sale Price}$$
  The accuracy of this computation is governed by OIML R-76 Clause 4.14 (*Price computing instruments*) and Seventh Schedule Heading A. The legal definitions of Unit Sale Price promulgated under G.S.R. 226(E) and deferred under G.S.R. 60(E) directly dictate the rounding, decimal precision, and display units required in price-computing NAWI test reports.

---

## 1. Executive Summary & Legal Architecture

### A. Statutory Core Mandate:
*The Legal Metrology (Packaged Commodities) Amendment Rules, 2023* enacted comprehensive structural reforms clarifying multi-product packaging classifications, e-commerce loose commodity deliveries, warranty servicing spare parts, and Unit Sale Price exemptions:

1. **New Package Classifications (Rule 2 Insertions):**
   - **Clause (ka) "Combination Package":** Package intended for retail sale containing two or more individual packages or pieces of **dissimilar commodities** (Illustration: package containing dissimilar commodities such as spoons, knives, forks, cups, napkins).
   - **Clause (kb) "Group Package":** Package intended for retail sale containing two or more individual packages or pieces of **similar, but not identical commodities** differing in weight, measure, volume, appearance, or quality (Illustrations: assorted sponges of differing dimensions; assorted biscuits; similar commodities of different brands).
   - **Clause (kc) "Multi-Piece Package":** Package containing two or more individual packaged or labelled pieces of the **same commodities of identical quantity**, intended for retail sale either individually or as a whole (Illustration: package containing 5 toilet soap cakes of net weight 20g each and total net weight of 100g). Food articles are safeguarded under FSSAI Act, 2006.

2. **Spare Parts & Warranty Servicing Relief (Rule 6(1)(d) Amendments — Effective 01-04-2024):**
   - Exemption from standard packaging declarations for spare parts and accessories used exclusively for warranty servicing and not sold to end customers.
   - For electronic products, spare parts, and accessories sold at retail, month and year of manufacture must be legibly marked anywhere on the retail package.

3. **Statutory Exemption from Unit Sale Price (USP) (Rule 6(11) Proviso):**
   - Explicitly stipulates that declaration of Unit Sale Price is **NOT required** for combination packages, group packages, or multi-piece packages.

4. **Loose Commodities Ordered via E-Commerce (Rule 26(g) Insertion):**
   - Regulates loose commodities ordered online where consumers know the ordered commodity, type, and quantity, mandating 4 clear declarations:
     (i) Name and address of manufacturer / marketer / brand owner / importer / seller with Country of Origin.
     (ii) Consumer care email ID and phone number.
     (iii) Retail sale price (MRP) in Indian currency inclusive of all taxes.
     (iv) **Net quantity in standard units of weight or measure**, or number if sold by count.

5. **Fourth Schedule Amendment (Item 11):**
   - Substituted declaration requirement: *"weight or volume and if the net quantity is declared by volume, the net quantity shall also be declared by weight"*.

### B. Direct Technical Linkage to SIH Problem Statement 26035 (NAWI OIML R-76):
- **Verification of Net Quantity for Pre-Packaged Goods:** Enforcement officers inspect pre-packages using standard Non-Automatic Weighing Instruments calibrated to OIML R-76 (Table 17 & Table 20 MPE limits). Multi-piece and combination packages require net quantity verification across individual pieces and total net weight.
- **Unit Sale Price Logic:** Software generating verification and inspection reports must recognize that multi-piece and combination packages are exempt from unit price computations, preventing false non-compliance flags during point-of-sale audits.

```text
सा.का.नि. 722(अ).—केंद्रीय सरकार, विधिक मापविज्ञान अधिनियम, 2009 (2010 का 1) की धारा 52 की उपधारा (2) के खंड (ञ) और (थ) के साथ पठित उपधारा (1) द्वारा प्रदत्त शक्तियों का प्रयोग करते हुए विधिक मापविज्ञान (पैकेट में रखी वस्तुएं) नियम, 2011 में और संशोधन करने के लिए निम्नलिखित नियम बनाती है, अर्थात्:-

1. संक्षिप्त नाम और प्रारंभ–
   (1) इन नियमों का संक्षिप्त नाम विधिक मापविज्ञान (पैकेट में रखी वस्तुएं) संशोधन नियम, 2023 है।
   (2) जैसा अन्यथा उपबंधित है इसके सिवाय, ये नियम 1 जनवरी 2024 से प्रवृत्त होंगे।

2. विधिक मापविज्ञान (पैकेट में रखी वस्तुएं) नियम, 2011 में, (जिसे इसमें इसके पश्चात् उक्त नियम कहा गया है) नियम 2 में, खंड (ट) के पश्चात् निम्नलिखित खंड अंतर्विष्ट किया जाएगा, अर्थात्:-

   ‘(ट, क) "संयोजन पैकेज" से खुदरा बिक्री के लिए पैकेज, जिसमें भिन्न वस्तुओं के दो या अधिक, अलग-अलग पैकेज, या अलग-अलग वस्तुएं होती हैं, अभिप्रेत है;
   दृष्टांत: पैकेज जिसमें चम्मच, चाकू, कांटे, कप, नैपकिन या इसी तरह की अन्य अलग-अलग वस्तुएं अंतर्विष्ट हैं, वाला एक संयोजन पैकेज है;

   (ट, ख) "ग्रुप पैकेज" से खुदरा बिक्री के लिए एक पैकेज, जिसमें दो या दो से अधिक अलग-अलग पैकेज, या अलग-अलग वस्तुएं होती हैं, जो समान हैं, लेकिन एक जैसी नहीं हैं (चाहे मात्रा में या आकार में) अभिप्रेत है।
   स्पष्टीकरण: ऐसी वस्तुएं जो सामान्यतः एक जैसी होती हैं लेकिन वजन, माप या आयतन, रूप या गुणवत्ता में भिन्न होती हैं, समान होती हैं, लेकिन एक जैसी वस्तुएं नहीं होती हैं।
   दृष्टांत:
   (i) एक पैकेज जिसमें शामिल हैं,-
   दो स्पंज - 100 मिमी x 75 मिमी x 20 मिमी
   एक स्पंज - 100 मिमी x 100 मिमी x 30 मिमी
   चार स्पंज - 100 मिमी x 50 मिमी x 15 मिमी
   एक ग्रुप पैकेज है;
   (ii) मिश्रित बिस्कुट वाला पैकेज एक ग्रुप पैकेज है;
   (iii) विभिन्न ब्रांडों की समान वस्तुओं वाला पैकेज एक ग्रुप पैकेज है;

   (ट, ग) "मल्टी-पीस पैकेज" से खुदरा बिक्री के लिए समान मात्रा की एक ही वस्तु के दो या दो से अधिक अलग-अलग पैकेज या लेबल वाले टुकड़ों वाला पैकेज अभिप्रेत है, जो व्यक्तिगत टुकड़ों में या पूरे पैकेज के रूप में अभिप्रेत है:
   परंतु यह कि खाद्य सामग्री वाले पैकेजों के लिए, खाद्य सुरक्षा और मानक अधिनियम, 2006 (2006 का 34) और उसके अधीन बनाए गए नियमों के उपबंध लागू होंगे।
   दृष्टांत:
   (i) "प्रत्येक 20 ग्राम शुद्ध वजन वाले और 100 ग्राम के कुल शुद्ध वजन वाले 5 टॉयलेट साबुन की टिकिया" वाला पैकेज एक मल्टी-पीस पैकेज है;’;

3. उक्त नियमों में, नियम 6 में, उप-नियम (1) में, खंड (घ) में तीसरे परंतुक के पश्चात्, 1 अप्रैल, 2024 से निम्नलिखित अंतःस्थापित किया जाएगा, अर्थात्:-
   “परंतु यह भी कि इस खंड में निहित कुछ भी वारंटी के साथ सर्विसिंग के प्रयोजन के लिए उपयोग किए जाने वाले स्पेयर पार्ट्स एवं सहायक उपकरणों और अंतिम ग्राहक को विक्रय नहीं किए जाने पर लागू होंगे।
   परंतु यह भी कि इलेक्ट्रॉनिक उत्पादों, स्पेयर पार्ट्स और सहायक उपकरणों के लिए विनिर्माण के मास और वर्ष की घोषणा खुदरा पैकेज पर विशिष्टतः कहीं भी दृश्यमान और स्पष्ट रूप से सुपाठ्य रूप में होनी चाहिए।
   स्पष्टीकरण: इस खंड के प्रयोजन के लिए, “स्पेयर पार्ट्स और सहायक उपकरण में कोई भी “भाग”, “घटक” या “सहायक उपकरण जो किसी मशीन, उपकरण या उपस्कर के प्रमुख या मुख्य उत्पाद के पूरक हैं” चाहे किसी भी नाम से पुकारा जाए, इसमें वे पार्ट्स भी शामिल होंगे जो कि मशीन, उपकरण या उपस्कर के इच्छित प्रचालन या क्षतिग्रस्त या घिसे-पिटे हिस्से को समर्थित करने या बदलने के लिए उपयोग करने के लिए पृथक रूप से विक्रय किए जाते हैं।”

4. उक्त नियम में, नियम 6 में, उप-नियम (11) में, दूसरे परंतुक के पश्चात्, निम्नलिखित परंतुक अंत:स्थापित किया जाएगा, अर्थात्:-
   “परंतु यह भी कि इन नियमों या तत्समय प्रवृत्त किसी अन्य विधि के अधीन परिभाषित संयोजन पैकेज या ग्रुप पैकेज या मल्टीपीस पैकेज के लिए यूनिट बिक्री मूल्य की घोषणा की आवश्यकता नहीं है।”

5. उक्त नियमों में, नियम 26 में, खंड (च) में, प्रथम परंतुक के पश्चात, मद (iii) के स्थान पर निम्नलिखित परंतुक को अंतःस्थापित किया जाएगा, अर्थात्:-
   “(iii) सेंटीमीटर या मीटर के संदर्भ मीट्रिक अंकन में उत्पाद का आकार जैसा भी मामला हो या के साथ अंतरराष्ट्रीय स्तर पर पहचाने जाने योग्य आकार संकेतकों जैसे कि एस, एम, एल, एक्सएल, एक्सएक्सएल और एक्सएक्सएक्सएल के साथ सेंटीमीटर या मीटर के संदर्भ में, या जैसा भी मामला हो मीट्रिक अंकन में विवरण के साथ हो सकता है।”

6. उक्त नियमों में, नियम 26 में, खंड (च) के पश्चात, निम्नलिखित खंड अंतःस्थापित किया जाएगा, अर्थात्:-
   “(छ) इसमें ई-कॉमर्स चैनलों के माध्यम से ऑर्डर की गई खुली वस्तुएं शामिल है, जहां उपभोक्ता ऑर्डर की गई वस्तु, उसके प्रकार और मात्रा के बारे में जानते हैं;
   परंतु ऐसी वस्तुओं को निम्नलिखित जानकारी वहन करनी होगी, अर्थात्:-
   (i) आयात किए गए उत्पादों के मामले में मूल देश या विनिर्माण देश के साथ विनिर्माता या विपणक या ब्रांड के मालिक या आयातक या विक्रेता का नाम और पता;
   (ii) कंज्यूमर केयर ई-मेल आईडी और फोन नंबर;
   (iii) भारतीय मुद्रा में सभी करों सहित पैकेज का अधिकतम खुदरा मूल्य; और
   (iv) शुद्ध मात्रा, बाट या माप की मानक इकाई के संदर्भ में, या जहां वस्तु संख्या, वस्तु की संख्या द्वारा विक्रय की जाती है।”

7. उक्त नियमों में, चौथी अनुसूची में, क्रम संख्या (11) के सामने, स्तम्भ 3 में प्रविष्टि के स्थान पर “वजन या आयतन और यदि शुद्ध मात्रा आयतन द्वारा घोषित की जाती है तो शुद्ध मात्रा वजन द्वारा भी घोषित की जाएगी’’ प्रविष्टि को रखा जाएगा।

[फा. सं. आई-10/22/2021 डब्लल्यू एंड एम]
निधि खरे, विशेष सचिव

टिप्पण: मूल नियम भारत के राजपत्र, असाधारण, भाग II, खंड 3, उप-खंड (i) में सा.का.नि. 202 (अ) तारीख 7 मार्च, 2011 द्वारा प्रकाशित किए गए थे और अधिसूचना संख्या सा.का.नि. 714(अ) तारीख 30 सितम्बर 2023 द्वारा अंतिम बार संशोधित किए गए थे।
```

## 3. Complete Verbatim English Text (The Legal Metrology (Packaged Commodities) Amendment Rules, 2023)

```text
MINISTRY OF CONSUMER AFFAIRS, FOOD AND PUBLIC DISTRIBUTION
(Department of Consumer Affairs)
NOTIFICATION
New Delhi, the 6th October, 2023

G.S.R. 722(E).—In exercise of the powers conferred by sub-section (1), read with clauses (j) and (q) of sub-section (2) of section 52 of the Legal Metrology Act, 2009 (1 of 2010), the Central Government hereby makes the following rules further to amend the Legal Metrology (Packaged Commodities) Rules, 2011, namely:-

1. Short title and commencement. – (1) These rules may be called the Legal Metrology (Packaged Commodities) Amendment Rules, 2023.
   (2) Save as otherwise provided, these rules shall come into force on the 1st day of January, 2024.

2. In the Legal Metrology (Packaged Commodities) Rules, 2011 (hereinafter referred to as the said rules), in rule 2, after clause (k), the following clauses shall be inserted, namely:-
   ‘(ka) "combination package" means a package intended for retail sale, containing two or more individual packages, or individual pieces, of dissimilar commodities;
   Illustration: A package containing dissimilar commodities such as, spoons, knives, forks, cups, napkins, or the like is a combination package;

   (kb) "group package" means a package intended for retail sale, containing two or more individual packages, or individual pieces, of similar, but not identical (whether in quantity or size) commodities.
   Explanation: Commodities which are generally the same but differ in weight, measure or volume, appearance or quality are similar, but not identical commodities.
   Illustrations:
   (i) A package containing,-
   Two sponges -100 mmx 75 mm x 20 mm
   One sponge -100 mmx 100 mm x 30 mm
   Four sponges -100 mmx 50 mm x 15 mm
   is a group package;
   (ii) A package containing assorted biscuits is a group package;
   (iii) A package containing similar commodities of different brands is a group package;

   (kc) "multi-piece package" means a package containing two or more individual packaged or labelled pieces of the same commodities of identical quantity, intended for retail sale, either in individual pieces or the package as a whole:
   Provided that for packages containing food articles, the provisions of the Food Safety and Standards Act, 2006 (34 of 2006) and the rules made thereunder shall apply.
   Illustration:
   (i) A package containing "5 toilet soap cakes of net weight 20g each and total net weight of 100 g" is a multi-piece package;’;

3. In the said rules, in rule 6, in sub-rule (1), in clause (d) after the third proviso, the following shall be inserted with effect from the 1st day of April, 2024, namely:-
   “Provided also that nothing contained in this clause shall apply to spare parts and accessories used for the purpose of servicing with a warranty and not sold to end customers:
   Provided also that for electronic products, spare parts and accessories, the declaration of month and year of manufacture shall be specified anywhere on the retail package in a visible and clearly legible manner.
   Explanation: For the purpose of this clause, “spare parts and accessories shall include any “part”, “component”, or “accessories that are complementary to the main or core product” of a machine, device or equipment, by whatever name called, including parts that are sold separately for use in support or replacement of a damaged or worn-out part, in order to have intended operation or functioning of the machine, device or equipment.".

4. In the said rules, in rule 6, in sub-rule (11), after the second proviso, the following proviso shall be inserted, namely:-
   “Provided also that declaration of unit sale price is not required for a combination package or a group package or a multi-piece package, under these rules or under any other law for the time being in force.”.

5. In the said rules, in rule 26, in clause (f), in the first proviso, for item (iii), the following item shall be substituted, namely:-
   “(iii) size of the product in metric notation in terms of cm or m, as the case may be, or with internationally recognizable size indicators such as S, M, L, XL, XXL and XXXL along with details in metric notation in the terms of cm or m, as the case may be;”.

6. In the said rules, in rule 26, after clause (f), the following clause shall be inserted, namely:-
   “(g) it contains loose commodities ordered through e-commerce channels, where consumers are aware of the ordered commodity, its type and quantity:
   Provided that such commodities shall bear the following information, namely:-
   (i) name and address of manufacturer or marketer or brand owner or importer or seller with the country of origin or manufacture in the case of imported products;
   (ii) consumer care email id and phone number;
   (iii) retail sale price of the package inclusive of all taxes in the Indian currency; and
   (iv) net quantity, in terms of the standard unit of weight or measure, or where the commodity is sold by number, the number of the commodity.”.

7. In the said rules, in the FOURTH SCHEDULE, against serial number (11), for the entry in column 3, the entry “weight or volume and if the net quantity is declared by volume, the net quantity shall also be declared by weight” shall be substituted.

[F. No. I-10/22/2021-W&M]
NIDHI KHARE, Special Secy.

Note: The principal rules were published in the Gazette of India, Extraordinary, Part II, Section 3, Sub-section (i) vide G.S.R. number 202 (E), dated the 7th March, 2011 and was last amended vide number G.S.R. 714 (E), dated 30th September, 2023.

Uploaded by Dte. of Printing at Government of India Press, Ring Road, Mayapuri, New Delhi-110064
and Published by the Controller of Publications, Delhi-110054.
MANOJ KUMAR VERMA
Digitally signed by MANOJ KUMAR VERMA
Date: 2023.10.06 22:15:33 +05'30'
```

# Complete Extraction: The Legal Metrology (Packaged Commodities) (Amendment) Rules, 2023 (G.S.R. 412(E))

**File:** `dataset/2023_06_5_amendment_in_amendment_of_PCR_ext_till_30_6_2023_0_1732709179.pdf` (2 Pages — 100% Direct Digital Text)  
**Gazette Identifier:** REGD. No. D. L.-33004/99 | CG-DL-E-05062023-246281 | The Gazette of India Extraordinary, Part II—Section 3—Sub-section (i) | सं. 338 / No. 338 | Press Imprint: 3515 GI/2023  
**Notification Number:** G.S.R. 412(E) / सा.का.नि. 412(अ)  
**Date of Notification:** New Delhi, the 5th June, 2023 / नई दिल्ली, 5 जून, 2023  
**Date of Gazette Publication:** Monday, June 5, 2023 / Jyaishtha 15, 1945 (सोमवार, जून 5, 2023/ज्येष्ठ 15, 1945)  
**Ministry:** Ministry of Consumer Affairs, Food and Public Distribution (Department of Consumer Affairs) / उपभोक्ता मामले, खाद्य और सार्वजनिक वितरण मंत्रालय (उपभोक्ता मामले विभाग)  
**Statutory Authority:** Sub-section (1), read with clauses (j) and (q) of sub-section (2), of Section 52 of the Legal Metrology Act, 2009 (1 of 2010) [विधिक मापविज्ञान अधिनियम, 2009 (2010 का 1) की धारा 52 की उपधारा (2) के खंड (ञ) और खंड (थ) के साथ पठित उपधारा (1)]  
**Signatory:** Nidhi Khare, Additional Secretary [F. No. I-10/22/2021-WM] / निधि खरे, अपर सचिव [फा. सं. I-10/22/2021-डब्ल्यू एम]  
**Target Statutory Rules:** *The Legal Metrology (Packaged Commodities) Amendment Rules, 2022* (originally notified vide G.S.R. 226(E) dated 28th March, 2022 — Document 10 in this knowledge base; last amended vide G.S.R. 214(E) dated 24th March, 2023).  
**Enforcement Date:** Immediate on date of publication in the Official Gazette (5th June, 2023).

---

## 1. Executive Summary & Legal Architecture

### A. Statutory Core Mandate:
*The Legal Metrology (Packaged Commodities) (Amendment) Rules, 2023* enacted a further intermediate extension of the compliance transition window for the Unit Sale Price (USP) and Second Schedule omission provisions originally promulgated under **G.S.R. 226(E)**:
1. **Rule 1(2) Amendment:**
   Substitutes the commencement date in Rule 1, sub-rule (2) of the 2022 Amendment Rules:
   > For the figures, letters, and words *“1st day of June, 2023”*, the figures, letters, and words **“1st day of July, 2023”** shall be substituted.
2. **Regulatory Continuity in Unit Sale Price Implementation:**
   The Central Government periodically recalibrated the implementation milestones of G.S.R. 226(E) to allow manufacturers, packaging converters, and retailers adequate time to exhaust pre-printed packaging materials bearing old declarations and recalibrate automated retail weighing and pricing systems:
   - 01-10-2022 (G.S.R. 226(E), Doc 10)
   - 01-12-2022 (G.S.R. 747(E), Doc 15)
   - 01-01-2023 (G.S.R. 859(E), Doc 14)
   - 01-02-2023 (G.S.R. 910(E))
   - 01-04-2023 (G.S.R. 60(E), Doc 34)
   - 01-06-2023 (G.S.R. 214(E))
   - **01-07-2023 (G.S.R. 412(E), Doc 36)**
   - 01-01-2024 (G.S.R. 714(E), Doc 11 & G.S.R. 722(E), Doc 35)

### B. Direct Technical Linkage to SIH Problem Statement 26035 (NAWI OIML R-76):
- **Pricing & Tare Cohesion in Retail Weighing Instruments:**
  Retail Non-Automatic Weighing Instruments integrated with barcode / thermal label printers compute unit pricing in accordance with OIML R-76 Clause 4.14. The statutory deferral under G.S.R. 412(E) ensured that point-of-sale pre-packaging software and label-printing NAWI devices were aligned with active legal enforcement dates.

## 1. Executive Summary & Legal Architecture

### A. Statutory Core Mandate:
*The Legal Metrology (Packaged Commodities) (Second Amendment) Rules, 2026* establish an extended statutory timeline for e-commerce entities to deploy specialized technical filtering infrastructure for imported goods:
1. **Rule 6(10A) Complete Substitution:**
   Substitutes sub-rule (10A) of Rule 6 (*Declarations to be made on every package*):
   > **“(10A) Every e-commerce entity offering for sale any imported product shall, with effect from the 1st day of July, 2027, ensure that the product listing of such imported product contains a searchable and sortable filter specifying the country of origin.”**
   > 
   > **“(10 क) प्रत्येक ई- वाणिज्य इकाई, जो किसी आयातित उत्पाद को विक्रय के लिए प्रस्थापित करती है, 1 जुलाई, 2027 से यह सुनिश्चित करेगी कि ऐसे आयातित उत्पाद की उत्पाद सूची में उद्भव के देश को विनिर्दिष्ट करने के लिए खोज योग्य और श्रेणी बद्धकरणीय फ़िल्टर अंतर्विष्ट हो ।”**
2. **Technological Mandate for E-Commerce Platforms:**
   - **Searchable Filter:** E-commerce marketplaces and direct sellers must index the Country of Origin (COO) within product search engines, allowing consumers to search by country name.
   - **Sortable Filter:** Platforms must provide faceted search/filtering categories (dropdowns/checkboxes) allowing users to filter product listings by specific countries of origin.
   - **Scope:** Applies unconditionally to all imported consumer products offered for sale online.
3. **Statutory Transition Period:**
   Sets the firm enforcement deadline as **1st July, 2027**, granting e-commerce portals sufficient development and catalog-tagging lead time to ensure compliance across millions of SKU listings.

---

### B. Direct Technical Linkage to SIH Problem Statement 26035 (NAWI OIML R-76):
1. **Metrological Equipment Supply Chain & E-Commerce Procurement:**
   - Non-automatic weighing instruments, electronic balances, load cells, indicators, and verification standard weights are increasingly procured via online B2B marketplaces and manufacturer e-commerce portals.
   - Under Rule 6(10A), e-commerce platforms selling imported weighing instruments or metrological components must implement searchable and sortable Country of Origin metadata.
2. **Model Approval & Verification Consistency:**
   - Every imported NAWI must possess a Model Approval certificate granted under Section 19 of the Legal Metrology Act, 2009.
   - The NAWI test report generation software must maintain strict traceability between the instrument’s declared **Country of Origin (COO)**, the importer’s registration, and the Indian Model Approval number issued by DoCA.
   - Software test certificate templates must validate that if an instrument is marked as imported, the Country of Origin field matches statutory declarations and pattern approval certificates.

```text
(1) These rules may be called the Legal Metrology (Packaged Commodities) Second Amendment Rules, 2026.
```

## 4. Analytical Cross-Reference: Evolution of E-Commerce Country of Origin (COO) Rules

| Statutory Rule | Notification Reference | Effective Date | E-Commerce Requirement |
| :--- | :--- | :--- | :--- |
| **Rule 6(10) (Original Provision)** | G.S.R. 202(E) / Amendment 2017 | Active | Mandatory display of Country of Origin on digital marketplace listing alongside MRP, net quantity, and manufacturer details. |
| **Rule 6(10A) (1st Amendment 2026)** | G.S.R. 128(E) (13 February, 2026) | Intermediate | Introduced requirement for searchable and sortable filters for imported products. |
| **Rule 6(10A) (2nd Amendment 2026)** *(This Document 40)* | **G.S.R. 312(E) (27 April, 2026)** | **1st July, 2027** | Finalized and extended the compliance enforcement date to **01-07-2027** for all e-commerce entities to deploy searchable and sortable Country of Origin filters. |

## 1. Executive Summary & Legal Architecture

### A. Statutory Core Mandate:
*The Legal Metrology (Packaged Commodities) Amendment Rules, 2026* represent the primary enactment that introduced mandatory Country of Origin (COO) filtering obligations for e-commerce platforms in India:
1. **Rule 6(10A) Original Insertion:**
   Inserts new sub-rule (10A) after sub-rule (10) of Rule 6 (*Declarations to be made on every package*):
   > **“(10A) Every e-commerce entity selling imported products shall provide the product listings of such imported products in a searchable and sortable filter specifying the country of origin.”**
   > 
   > **“(10क) आयातित उत्पादों का विक्रय करने वाली प्रत्येक ई-कॉमर्स इकाई ऐसे आयातित उत्पादों की उत्पाद सूचियां, मूल देश को निर्दिष्ट करने वाले खोज योग्य और क्रमबद्ध फ़िल्टर में उपलब्ध कराएगी”**
2. **Legislative Evolution & Inter-Document Connection:**
   - **Initial Insertion (G.S.R. 128(E) / This Document 41):**
     Promulgated on 13th February, 2026, setting the initial compliance enforcement date as **1st July, 2026**.
   - **Subsequent Modification (G.S.R. 312(E) / Document 40):**
     Promulgated on 27th April, 2026, substituting the phrasing to *"Every e-commerce entity offering for sale any imported product shall... ensure that the product listing of such imported product contains a searchable and sortable filter specifying the country of origin"* and extending the compliance date to **1st July, 2027**.

---

### B. Direct Technical Linkage to SIH Problem Statement 26035 (NAWI OIML R-76):
1. **Digital Metrology Cataloging & Component Traceability:**
   - Online catalogs and digital market platforms distributing weighing instruments, calibration test weights, load cells, and digital indicators must structure their database schemas to accommodate Country of Origin tagging.
2. **Verification Software Metadata Schema:**
   - In automated NAWI test report software, the instrument identification block must record:
     - `Country of Origin (COO)`
     - `Manufacturing Facility Location`
     - `Importer Registration Certificate Number` (under Section 23 of LM Act)
     - `Model Approval Certificate Number` (under Section 19 of LM Act)
   - The software ensures complete end-to-end traceability from the manufacturer's physical nameplate to the digital e-commerce listing and the final statutory verification certificate.

---

## 3. Complete Verbatim English Text (The Legal Metrology (Packaged Commodities) Amendment Rules, 2026)

```text
MINISTRY OF CONSUMER AFFAIRS, FOOD AND PUBLIC DISTRIBUTION 
(Department of Consumer Affairs) 
NOTIFICATION 
New Delhi, the 13th February, 2026 
 
G.S.R. 128(E).— In exercise of the powers conferred by sub-section (1) read with clauses (j) and (q) of sub-section (2) of section 52 of the Legal Metrology Act, 2009 (1 of 2010), the Central Government hereby makes the following rules further to amend the Legal Metrology (Packaged Commodities) Rules, 2011, namely:- 
  
1. Short title and commencement. – 
(1) These rules may be called the Legal Metrology (Packaged Commodities) Amendment Rules, 2026. 
(2) They shall come into force on the 1st day of July, 2026. 
  
2. In the Legal Metrology (Packaged Commodities) Rules, 2011, in rule 6, after sub-rule (10), the following sub-rule shall be inserted, namely:- 
  
“(10A) Every e-commerce entity selling imported products shall provide the product listings of such imported products in a searchable and sortable filter specifying the country of origin.”. 
  
[F. No. I-9/5/2023-W&M] 
ANUPAM MISHRA, Addl. Secy.  
  
Note: The principal rules were published in the Gazette of India, Extraordinary, Part II, Section 3, Sub-section (i) vide number G.S.R. 202 (E), dated the 7th March, 2011 and was last amended vide number G.S.R. 881(E), dated 2nd December, 2025. 
 
Uploaded by Dte. of Printing at Government of India Press, Ring Road, Mayapuri, New Delhi-110064 
and Published by the Controller of Publications, Delhi-110054.  
SARVESH KUMAR SRIVASTAVA 
Digitally signed by SARVESH KUMAR SRIVASTAVA 
Date: 2026.02.13 23:05:57 +05'30'
```

| Element | Original Insertion (G.S.R. 128(E) / Doc 41) | Substituted Version (G.S.R. 312(E) / Doc 40) |
| :--- | :--- | :--- |
| **Notification Date** | 13th February, 2026 | 27th April, 2026 |
| **Enforcement Date** | **1st July, 2026** | **1st July, 2027** (+1 Year Extension) |
| **Entity Obligation Scope** | *"selling imported products"* | *"offering for sale any imported product"* (Broader marketplace coverage) |
| **Platform Listing Requirement** | *"shall provide the product listings... in a searchable and sortable filter"* | *"shall ensure that the product listing of such imported product contains a searchable and sortable filter"* |
| **Devanagari Phrasing** | *"खोज योग्य और क्रमबद्ध फ़िल्टर"* | *"खोज योग्य और श्रेणी बद्धकरणीय फ़िल्टर"* |

## 1. Executive Summary & Legal Architecture

### A. Statutory Core Mandate:
*The Legal Metrology (Packaged Commodities) (Second Amendment) Rules, 2021* (G.S.R. 779(E)) represent a watershed structural reform in Indian packaging metrology, enacting consumer price transparency, pack size deregulation, and tax harmonization:
1. **Introduction of Mandatory Unit Sale Price (USP) (Rule 6(11)):**
   Mandates for the first time in India that every pre-packaged commodity display its **Unit Sale Price (USP)** alongside the Maximum Retail Price (MRP):
   - **Weight:**
     - Net quantity $< 1	ext{ kg}$: **`Rs.__ per g`**
     - Net quantity $\ge 1	ext{ kg}$: **`Rs.__ per kg`**
   - **Volume:**
     - Net volume $< 1	ext{ litre}$: **`Rs.__ per ml`**
     - Net volume $\ge 1	ext{ litre}$: **`Rs.__ per litre`**
   - **Length:**
     - Net length $< 1	ext{ metre}$: **`Rs.__ per cm`**
     - Net length $\ge 1	ext{ metre}$: **`Rs.__ per meter`**
   - **Count / Number:** **`Rs.__ per number`**
2. **Omission of Rule 5 & Deregulation of Pack Sizes:**
   Omitted Rule 5, which previously restricted manufacturers to rigid, mandatory standard packaging quantities prescribed in the Second Schedule. This deregulated pack sizing, granting industry packaging flexibility while protecting consumers through transparent Unit Sale Price declarations.
3. **Promotional Offers & Grouped Packages (Rule 4(2)):**
   Renumbered Rule 4 as Rule 4(1) and inserted Rule 4(2) establishing that when retail packages are grouped together for promotional offers, every individual package within the group must comply with all mandatory declarations under Rule 6.
4. **MRP in Indian Currency (Rule 6(1)(e)):**
   Streamlined MRP declaration text to require expression simple and unambiguous **"in Indian currency:"**, eliminating archaic, cumbersome statutory formatting.
5. **Sold by Number Quantity Declarations (Rule 13(5)(ii)):**
   Mandated that packages sold by number explicitly state: `number`, `unit`, `piece`, `pair`, `set`, or other words accurately expressing item quantity.
6. **Statutory Alignment with Consumer Protection Act, 2019 & GST (Rule 2(aa) & 18):**
   - Replaced obsolete references to the *Consumer Protection Act, 1986* with **Section 2(7)** (consumer) and **Section 2(41) & (47)** (unfair contract & unfair trade practice) of the *Consumer Protection Act, 2019 (35 of 2019)*.
   - Replaced obsolete state tax references (*Value Added Tax / VAT* or *Turn Over Tax / TOT*) with modern **Goods and Service Tax (GST)** under Rule 18(7).

---

### B. Direct Technical Linkage to SIH Problem Statement 26035 (NAWI OIML R-76):
1. **Price-Computing NAWI Algorithm Validation:**
   - Under OIML R-76 Section 4.13, Non-Automatic Weighing Instruments deployed at retail point-of-sale incorporate price-computing and price-labeling mechanisms.
   - Price-computing scales dynamically multiply measured net weight by unit price to yield total price ($P_{\text{total}} = m_{\text{net}} \times P_{\text{unit}}$).
   - In accordance with Rule 6(11) of G.S.R. 779(E), the automated test software developed under SIH Problem Statement 26035 must evaluate that retail price-computing NAWI instruments correctly handle unit price inputs formatted in standard statutory units (`Rs./kg` or `Rs./g`), preventing illegal fractional rounding or non-standard metric representations.
2. **Pre-Package Net Quantity Gravimetric Checkweighing:**
   - NAWI Class II and Class III instruments are standard reference tools utilized by Legal Metrology Officers (LMOs) to perform tare and net quantity verification on pre-packaged goods.
   - Software test worksheets must incorporate gravimetric evaluation thresholds aligned with the 1 kg and 1 litre cut-offs established under Rule 6(11).

```text
3. उक्त नियम में, नियम 4 को उसके उप-नियम (1) के रूप में पुनसंख्यांकित किया जाएगा, और उप-नियम (1) के रूप में इस प्रकार पुनसंख्यांकित किए जाने के पश्चात्, निम्नलिखित उप-नियम अंतःस्थापित किया जाएगा, अर्थात्:- 
"(2) जब खुदरा बिक्री के लिए आशयित एक या अधिक पैकेज प्रोत्साहन संबंधी प्रस्ताव पर खुदरा पैकेज के रूप में बेचे जाने के लिए एक साथ समूहित किए जाते हैं, तो समूह का प्रत्येक पैकेज नियम 6 के उपबंधों का पालन करेगा।" 
 
4. उक्त नियम में नियम 5 का लोप किया जाएगा। 
 
5. उक्त नियम में नियम 6 के उपनियम (1) में,-- 
(i) खंड (घ) में, "या पहले से पैक किया गया अथवा आयात किया गया; " शब्दों का लोप किया जाएगा; 
(ii) खंड (ड.) में, "और रुपयों अथवा पैसों में कीमत" से आरंभ होने वाले, और "(घ) एमआरपी रुपये xx.xx (सभी करों सहित)’’ से समाप्त होने वाले शब्दों के स्थान पर, "भारतीय मुद्रा में, " शब्द रखे जाएंगे; 
(iii) उप-नियम (10) के पश्चात्, निम्नलिखित उप-नियम अंतःस्थापित किया जाएगा, अर्थात्:- 
  "(11) इकाई बिक्री मूल्य निम्नलिखित के रूप में घोषित किया जाएगा- 
(i) एक किलोग्राम से कम की वस्तु की शुद्ध मात्रा वाली पहले से पैक की गई वस्तुओं के लिए "रु.__ प्रति ग्राम"; 
(ii) एक किलोग्राम से अधिक या उसके बराबर की वस्तु की शुद्ध मात्रा के साथ पहले से पैक की गई वस्तुओं के लिए "रु.__ प्रति किलोग्राम"; 
(iii) एक मीटर से कम वस्तु की शुद्ध लंबाई वाली पहले से पैक की गई वस्तुओं के लिए "रु.__ प्रति सेमी"; 
(iv) एक मीटर से अधिक या उसके बराबर की वस्तु की शुद्ध लंबाई वाली पहले से पैक की गई वस्तुओं के लिए "रु.__ प्रति मीटर"; 
(v) "रु.__ प्रति संख्या"; 
(vi) एक लीटर से कम की वस्तु की शुद्ध मात्रा के साथ पहले से पैक की गई वस्तुओं के लिए "रु.__ प्रति मिली लीटर"; 
(vii) एक लीटर से अधिक या उसके बराबर वस्तु की शुद्ध मात्रा के साथ पहले से पैक की गई वस्तुओं के लिए "रु.__ प्रति लीटर"। 
 
6. उक्त नियम के नियम 13 में उपनियम (5) के खंड (ii) के स्थान पर निम्नलिखित खंड रखा जाएगा, अर्थात्:- 
"(ii) संख्या द्वारा बेची गई मदों के लिए, संख्या या इकाई या टुकड़ा या जोड़ी या सेट या ऐसे अन्य शब्द जो पैक में मात्रा का वर्णन करते हैं, का उल्लेख किया जाएगा।"। 
 
7. उक्त नियम के नियम 18 में,- 
(i) उप-नियम (2क) में, ‘‘उपभोक्ता संरक्षण अधिनियम, 1986 (1986 का 68) की धारा 2 की उप-धारा (1) के क्रमशः खंड (ढढढ) और खंड (द)’’ शब्दों, कोष्ठकों, अक्षरों और अंकों के स्थान पर, "उपभोक्ता संरक्षण अधिनियम, 2019 (2019 का 35) की धारा 2 के खंड (41) और खंड (47)" शब्द, कोष्ठक, अक्षर और अंक रखे जाएंगे; 
(ii) उप-नियम (7) में, शब्दों, कोष्ठकों और अक्षरों, "मूल्य वर्धित कर (वैट) अथवा टर्न ओवर टैक्स (टीओटी)" के स्थान पर, "वस्तु और सेवा कर" शब्द रखे जाएंगे। 
 
[फा. सं. आई-10/22/2021]
```
