## 3. Complete Verbatim English Text (The Legal Metrology (Packaged Commodities) Amendment Rules, 2021)

```text
MINISTRY OF CONSUMER AFFAIRS, FOOD AND PUBLIC DISTRIBUTION 
(Department of Consumer Affairs) 
NOTIFICATION 
New Delhi, the 2nd November, 2021 
    
G.S.R. 779(E).––In exercise of the powers conferred by sub-section (1) read with clause (j) and (q) of sub-section (2) of section 52 of the Legal Metrology Act 2009, (1 of 2010), the Central Government hereby makes the following rules further to amend the Legal Metrology (Packaged Commodities) Rules, 2011, namely:– 
 
1. Short title and commencement.–(1) These rules may be called the Legal Metrology (Packaged Commodities) Amendment Rules, 2021. 
(2) They shall come into force on the 1st day of April, 2022.  
 
2. In the Legal Metrology (Packaged Commodities) Rules, 2011 (hereinafter referred to as the said rules), in rule 2, in clause (aa), for the words, brackets, letters and figures ―clause (d) of sub-section (1) of section 2 of the Consumer Protection Act, 1986 (68 of 1986)‖, the words, brackets, letters and figures ―clause (7) of section 2 of the Consumer Protection Act, 2019 (35 of 2019)‖ shall be substituted. 
 
3. In the said rules, rule 4 shall be renumbered as sub-rule (1) thereof, and after sub-rule (1) as so renumbered, the following sub-rule shall be inserted, namely:–– 
―(2) When one or more packages intended for retail sale are grouped together for being sold as a retail package on promotional offer, every package of the group shall comply with provisions of rule 6.‖. 
 
4. In the said rules, rule 5 shall be omitted. 
 
5. In the said rules, in rule 6, in sub-rule (1),–– 
(i) in clause (d), the words, ―or pre-packed or imported‖ shall be omitted; 
(ii) in clause (e), for the words commencing with ―and the price in rupees and paisa‖, and ending with ―(d) MRP Rs. ₹ xx.xx(inclusive of all taxes):‖, the words ―in Indian currency:‖ shall be substituted; 
(iii) after sub-rule (10), the following sub-rule shall be inserted, namely:–– 
 
―(11) The unit sale price shall be declared as– 
(i) ―Rs.__ per g‖ for pre-packaged commodities with net quantity of commodity less than one kilogram;  
(ii) ―Rs.__ per kg‖ for pre-packaged commodities with net quantity of commodity more or equal to one kilogram; 
(iii) ―Rs.__ per cm‖ for pre-packaged commodities with net length of the commodity less than one meter; 
(iv) ―Rs.__ per meter‖ for pre-packaged commodities with net length of the commodity more or equal to one meter; 
(v) ―Rs.__ per number‖;    
(vi) ―Rs.__ per ml‖ for pre-packaged commodities with net volume of the commodity less than one litre;  
(vii) ―Rs.__ per litre‖ for pre-packaged commodities with net volume of the commodity more or equal to one litre.‖. 
 
6. In the said rules, in rule 13, in sub-rule (5), for clause (ii), the following clause shall be substituted, namely:–  
―(ii) for items sold by number, the number or unit or piece or pair or set or such other word which represents the quantity in the package shall be mentioned.‖.  
 
7. In the said rules, in rule 18,– 
(i) in sub-rule (2A), for the words, brackets, letters and figures, ―clause (nnn) and clause (r), respectively of sub-section (1) of section 2 of the Consumer Protection Act, 1986 (68 of 1986)‖, the words, brackets, letters and figures ―clause (41) and (47) of section 2 of the Consumer Protection Act, 2019 (35 of 2019)‖ shall be substituted; 
(ii) in sub-rule (7), for the words, brackets and letters, ―Value Added Tax (VAT) or Turn Over Tax (TOT)‖, the words, ―Goods and Service Tax‖ shall be substituted.     
 
[F. No. I-10/22/2021] 
ANUPAM MISHRA, Jt. Secy.
```

## 1. Executive Summary & Legal Architecture

### A. Statutory Core Mandate:
*The Legal Metrology (Packaged Commodities) (Amendment) Rules, 2023* (G.S.R. 640(E)) represent a vital transitional extension notification promulgated by the Department of Consumer Affairs:
1. **Rule 1(2) Substitution (Extended Commencement Date):**
   In the *Legal Metrology (Packaged Commodities) Amendment Rules, 2022* (G.S.R. 226(E)), in Rule 1, in sub-rule (2):
   > **“For the figures, letters and words ‘1st day of September, 2023’, the figures, letters and words ‘1st day of October, 2023’ shall be substituted.”**
   > 
   > **“अंकों, अक्षरों और शब्दों ‘1 सितंबर 2023’ के स्थान पर अंक, अक्षर और शब्द ‘1 अक्टूबर 2023’ रखा जाएगा।”**
2. **Regulatory Relief & Inventory Exhaustion:**
   Provided a one-month transitional extension from 1st September, 2023 to **1st October, 2023** for electronic products, packaged commodities, and industry stakeholders to transition packaging lines, deplete pre-printed inventory, and ensure complete compliance with QR code labeling and packaging declarations.

---

### B. Direct Technical Linkage to SIH Problem Statement 26035 (NAWI OIML R-76):
1. **Packaging Metrology & Electronic Weighing Instrument Shipments:**
   - Electronic weighing instruments (scales, indicators, laboratory balances) distributed in packaged cartons fall under the dual purview of the *Legal Metrology (General) Rules, 2011* (technical verification and pattern approval) and the *Legal Metrology (Packaged Commodities) Rules, 2011* (outer carton labelling, net quantity, manufacturer address, and QR codes).
   - The NAWI test report application must track transitional packaging enforcement dates to ensure that historical compliance audits and verification certificate issuance reflect the exact statutory timeline in force at the time of manufacture and packaging.

---

# Complete Extraction: The Legal Metrology (Packaged Commodities) (Amendment) Rules, 2025 (G.S.R. 778(E))

**Notification Number:** G.S.R. 778(E) / सा.का.नि. 778(अ)  
**Date of Notification:** New Delhi, the 23rd October, 2025 / नई दिल्ली, 23 अक्तूबर, 2025  
**Date of Gazette Publication:** Friday, October 24, 2025 / Kartika 2, 1947 (शुक्रवार, अक्तूबर 24, 2025/कार्तिक 2, 1947)  
**Ministry:** Ministry of Consumer Affairs, Food and Public Distribution (Department of Consumer Affairs) / उपभोक्ता मामले, खाद्य और सार्वजनिक वितरण मंत्रालय (उपभोक्ता मामले विभाग)  
**Statutory Authority:** Sub-section (1) read with clauses (j) and (q) of sub-section (2) of Section 52 of the Legal Metrology Act, 2009 (1 of 2010) [विधिक मापविज्ञान अधिनियम, 2009 (2010 का 1) की धारा 52 की उपधारा (2) के खंड (ञ) और (थ) द्वारा प्रदत्त शक्तियों का प्रयोग करते हुए]  
**Signatory:** Anupam Mishra, Joint Secretary [F. No. I-19/33/2022-W&M] / अनुपम मिश्रा, संयुक्त सचिव [फा. सं. I-19/33/2022 डब्लल्यू एंड एम]  
**Target Principal Rules:** *The Legal Metrology (Packaged Commodities) Rules, 2011* [विधिक मापविज्ञान (पैक में रखी वस्तुएं) नियम, 2011], originally published vide notification number G.S.R. 202(E), dated 7th March, 2011 and last amended vide G.S.R. 722(E), dated 6th October, 2023.  
**Statutory Enforcement Date:** Immediate on date of publication in the Official Gazette (23rd/24th October, 2025).  
**Digital Signature:** Sarvesh Kumar Srivastava, Date: 2025.10.24 21:05:48 +05'30'  

---

## 1. Executive Summary & Legal Architecture

### A. Statutory Core Mandate:
*The Legal Metrology (Packaged Commodities) (Amendment) Rules, 2025* (G.S.R. 778(E)) enact a comprehensive statutory harmonization between the *Legal Metrology (Packaged Commodities) Rules, 2011* and the *Medical Devices Rules, 2017* (MDR 2017), resolving dual-regulatory conflicts for medical instruments and devices:
1. **Rule 2(h) Proviso Insertion (Medical Devices Rules Precedence):**
   In Rule 2, to clause (h) (*declarations on packages*):
   > **“Provided that for packages containing medical devices, the provisions of the Medical Devices Rules, 2017, shall apply to make declarations;”**
   > 
   > **“परंतु यह कि चिकित्सा युक्तियां अंतर्विष्ट करने वाले पैकेजों के लिए, घोषणाएं करने हेतु चिकित्सा युक्ति नियम, 2017 के उपबंध लागू होंगे;”**
2. **Rule 7(2) & 7(3) Font Dimension Harmonization:**
   In Rule 7 (*Principles of display of declaration*):
   - **Numeral and Letter Height (Rule 7(2)):**
     Inserts a specific proviso establishing that for packages containing medical devices, font height provisions shall be governed exclusively by the *Medical Devices Rules, 2017*.
   - **Numeral and Letter Width (Rule 7(3)):**
     Inserts a matching proviso establishing that font width specifications shall also be governed by the *Medical Devices Rules, 2017*.
3. **Rule 33 Amendment (Exclusion of Power to Relax for Medical Devices):**
   - Renumbers Rule 33 (*Power to relax*) as sub-rule (1).
   - Inserts sub-rule (2):
     > **“(2) In case where the Medical Devices Rules, 2017 are applicable, the relaxation given under this rule shall not apply.”**
     > 
     > **“(2) उस दशा में जहां चिकित्सा युक्ति नियम, 2017 लागू होते हैं, इस नियम के अधीन दिया गया शिथिलीकरण लागू नहीं होगा।”**
   - Prohibits the Central Government from granting arbitrary administrative relaxations or waivers under the Legal Metrology regime where specialized medical safety regulations apply under MDR 2017.

---

### B. Direct Technical Linkage to SIH Problem Statement 26035 (NAWI OIML R-76):
1. **Medical NAWI Category Classification:**
   - Under OIML R-76, certain Non-Automatic Weighing Instruments are designed specifically for clinical and medical applications: infant incubators with integrated scales, baby scales, bed scales, patient lift scales, and precision pharmaceutical balances.
   - These instruments frequently hold dual status as both legal weighing instruments (subject to OIML R-76 pattern approval and verification) and regulated medical devices under the *Medical Devices Rules, 2017*.
2. **Automated Inspection Checklist & Labeling Validation:**
   - When the SIH 26035 software evaluates a medical weighing instrument or its outer packaging:
     - The software's packaging inspection checklist dynamically defers font size, height, width, and mandatory warning declarations to the *Medical Devices Rules, 2017*.
     - The verification engine flags that statutory relaxations under Rule 33 cannot be applied to medical weighing devices, ensuring strict regulatory integrity before generating test certificates.

---

```text
3. मूल नियमों के, नियम 7 में, -  
(i) उप-नियम (2) में, अंत में निम्नलिखित परंतुक अंत:स्थापित किया जाएगा, अर्थात्: -  
"परंतु यह कि चिकित्सा युक्तियां अंतर्विष्ट करने वाले पैकेजों के लिए, घोषणा करने हेतु किसी भी अंक और अक्षर की ऊंचाई के लिए, चिकित्सा युक्ति नियम, 2017 के उपबंध लागू होंगे।";  
(ii) उप-नियम (3) में, अंत में निम्नलिखित परंतुक अंत:स्थापित किया जाएगा, अर्थात्: -  
"परंतु यह कि चिकित्सा युक्तियां अंतर्विष्ट करने वाले पैकेजों के लिए, घोषणा करने हेतु किसी भी अंक और अक्षर की चौडाई के लिए, चिकित्सा युक्ति नियम, 2017 के उपबंध लागू होंगे।"  
 
4. मूल नियमों में, नियम 33 उप-नियम (1) के रूप में संख्यांकित होगा और इस प्रकार संख्यांकित नियमों के पश्चात्, निम्नलिखित उप-नियम अंत:स्थापित किया जाएगा, अर्थात्: -  
"(2) उस दशा में जहां चिकित्सा युक्ति नियम, 2017 लागू होते हैं, इस नियम के अधीन दिया गया शिथिलीकरण लागू नहीं होगा।” 
 
[फा. सं. I-19/33/2022 डब्लल्यू एंड एम] 
अनुपम मिश्रा, संयुक्त सचिव 
 
टिप्पण : मूल नियम, भारत के राजपत्र, असाधारण, भाग II, खंड 3, उपखंड (i) में सा.का.नि. संख्या 202(अ), तारीख 7 मार्च, 2011 द्वारा प्रकाशित किए गए थे और अंतिम बार सा.का.नि. संख्या 722(अ) तारीख 6 अक्टूबर, 2023 द्वारा संशोधित किए गए थे।
```

## 3. Complete Verbatim English Text (The Legal Metrology (Packaged Commodities) Amendment Rules, 2025)

```text
MINISTRY OF CONSUMER AFFAIRS, FOOD AND PUBLIC DISTRIBUTION 
(Department of Consumer Affairs) 
NOTIFICATION 
New Delhi, the 23rd October, 2025. 
 
G.S.R. 778(E).—In exercise of the powers conferred by (j) and (q) of sub-section (2) of section 52 of the Legal Metrology Act, 2009 (1 of 2010), the Central Government hereby makes the following rules further to amend the Legal Metrology (Packaged Commodities) Rules, 2011, namely:- 
 
1. Short title and commencement. – 
(1) These rules may be called the Legal Metrology (Packaged Commodities) Amendment Rules, 2025. 
(2) They shall come into force on the date of their publication in the official gazette. 
 
2. In the Legal Metrology (Packaged Commodities) Rules, 2011 (hereinafter referred to as the principal rules), in rule 2, to clause (h), the following proviso shall be inserted, namely:- 
 
―Provided that for packages containing medical devices, the provisions of the Medical Devices Rules, 2017, shall apply to make declarations;‖. 
 
3. In the principal rules, in rule 7,- 
(i) in sub-rule (2), the following proviso shall be inserted at the end, namely:- 
 
―Provided that for packages containing medical devices, the provisions of the Medical Devices Rules, 2017, shall apply for the height of any numeral and letter to make declarations.‖; 
 
(ii) to sub-rule (3), the following proviso shall be inserted at the end, namely:- 
 
―Provided that for packages containing medical devices, the provisions of the Medical Devices Rules, 2017, shall apply for the width of any numeral and letter to make declarations.‖. 
 
[भाग II—खण्ड 3(i)]

―(2) In case where the Medical Devices Rules, 2017 are applicable, the relaxation given under this rule shall not apply.‖.  
 
[F. No. I-19/33/2022-W&M] 
ANUPAM MISHRA, Jt. Secy.
```

## 4. Analytical Cross-Reference: Medical Devices vs Packaged Commodities Rules Precedence

| Regulatory Requirement | General Packaged Commodities Rule (PCR 2011) | Medical Devices Overriding Rule (G.S.R. 778(E) & MDR 2017) |
| :--- | :--- | :--- |
| **Mandatory Declarations on Outer Packaging** | Rule 2(h) & Rule 6 (Standard commercial declarations) | Medical Devices Rules, 2017 govern declarations on packages containing medical devices |
| **Numeral and Letter Height** | Rule 7(2) Table (Height based on net quantity area) | Medical Devices Rules, 2017 govern font height |
| **Numeral and Letter Width** | Rule 7(3) (Width proportional to height) | Medical Devices Rules, 2017 govern font width |
| **Power to Relax (Exemptions)** | Rule 33(1) (Central Govt may relax rules in public interest) | **Rule 33(2) prohibits any relaxation** where Medical Devices Rules, 2017 apply |

## 1. Executive Summary & Legal Architecture

### A. Statutory Core Mandate:
*The Legal Metrology (Packaged Commodities) (Second Amendment) Rules, 2025* (G.S.R. 881(E)) eliminate a major regulatory loophole in tobacco/masticatory retail packaging:
1. **Rule 26(a) Proviso Insertion (Pan Masala Exemption Withdrawal):**
   In Rule 26 (*Exemption in respect of certain packages*), clause (a) grants a general statutory exemption from mandatory packaging declarations for packages containing quantities of $10	ext{g}$ or $10	ext{ml}$ or less. G.S.R. 881(E) inserts a second proviso:
   > **“Provided further that the provisions of this clause shall not apply to pan masala.”**
   > 
   > **“परंतु यह और कि इस खंड के उपबंध पान मसाला पर लागू नहीं होंगे;”**
2. **Statutory Impact:**
   - Pan Masala sachets and pouches containing $10	ext{g}$ or less (e.g., $0.5	ext{g}, 1	ext{g}, 2	ext{g}, 4	ext{g}$) are completely stripped of the small-quantity exemption.
   - Every individual Pan Masala sachet must prominently display all statutory declarations under Rule 6:
     - Exact Net Quantity in grams.
     - Maximum Retail Price (MRP) in Indian currency.
     - Unit Sale Price (USP) in `Rs.__ per g`.
     - Name, address, and consumer care contact of manufacturer/packer.
     - Month and year of manufacture.
3. **Statutory Transition Timeline:**
   Mandated to come into force on **1st February, 2026**, allowing manufacturers two months to re-tool packaging lines and update pouch rotogravure printing cylinders.

---

### B. Direct Technical Linkage to SIH Problem Statement 26035 (NAWI OIML R-76):
1. **Class II High-Precision Gravimetric Verification:**
   - Verifying the declared net quantity on micro-sachets weighing $0.5	ext{g}$ to $5	ext{g}$ cannot be reliably performed on ordinary Class III commercial counter scales ($e \ge 1	ext{g}$).
   - Legal Metrology Officers must utilize **Class II High Accuracy Non-Automatic Weighing Instruments** (precision laboratory balances with verification scale interval $e = 1	ext{mg}$ or $e = 10	ext{mg}$) to carry out statutory net weight sampling and tare deduction.
2. **Automated Tolerances & MAD (Maximum Allowable Deficiency):**
   - The NAWI test report software developed under SIH Problem Statement 26035 incorporates pre-packaged commodity gravimetric sampling algorithms:
     - When inspecting commodities where small-package exemptions have been withdrawn (Pan Masala), the software guides the inspector through tare determination, gross mass logging, and net mass calculation at milligram accuracy.
     - Automatically flags violations against statutory Maximum Allowable Deficiencies (MAD) under the First Schedule of the Packaged Commodities Rules.

## 4. Analytical Cross-Reference: Small Quantity Exemption Architecture (Rule 26(a))

| Package Category | Net Quantity Threshold | Exemption Status under Rule 26(a) | Required Declarations |
| :--- | :--- | :--- | :--- |
| **General Pre-Packaged Goods** | $\le 10	ext{g}$ or $\le 10	ext{ml}$ | **Exempt** from individual package declarations | Declarations required only on master carton/outer wholesale package |
| **Pan Masala Sachets** | $\le 10	ext{g}$ (e.g. $0.5	ext{g}, 1	ext{g}, 2	ext{g}, 4	ext{g}$) | **NOT EXEMPT** (Proviso inserted by G.S.R. 881(E)) | **Mandatory on every individual sachet**: Net Qty, MRP, USP (`Rs./g`), Manufacturer details, Packaging date |
| **Weighing Metrology Requirement** | $0.5	ext{g}$ to $10	ext{g}$ sample checks | High precision Class II NAWI ($e \le 10	ext{mg}$) | Laboratory gravimetric tare & net content inspection worksheets |

```text
2. विधिक मापविज्ञान (पैकबंद वस्तुएं) संशोधन नियम, 2022 के नियम 1 के उप-नियम (2) में अंक, अक्षर और शब्द “1 अप्रैल 2023” के स्थान पर अंक, अक्षर और शब्द “1 जून 2023” रखा जाएगा।
```

```text
1.         (1) These rules may be called the Legal Metrology (Packaged Commodities) Amendment (Amendment)
```

## 3. Statutory & Metrological Significance

### A. The Evolution Chain of G.S.R. 226(E) Compliance Timeline
The Legal Metrology (Packaged Commodities) (Amendment) Rules, 2022 (G.S.R. 226(E)) instituted critical regulatory changes, including the electronic QR code declarations for electronic products and dimensional declarations for loose garments. Due to industry readiness and supply chain inventory transition, the Central Government executed an unprecedented series of transitional extensions:

| Order in Sequence | Amending Notification | Date of Gazette Notification | Substituted Compliance Date | Document Reference |
| :--- | :--- | :--- | :--- | :--- |
| **Original Enactment** | G.S.R. 226(E) | 28th March, 2022 | 1st October, 2022 | Document 7 / Document 43 ref |
| **1st Extension** | G.S.R. 577(E) | 14th July, 2022 | 1st December, 2022 | Historical Gazette Record |
| **2nd Extension** | G.S.R. 753(E) | 30th September, 2022 | 1st January, 2023 | Historical Gazette Record |
| **3rd Extension** | G.S.R. 60(E) | 27th January, 2023 | 1st April, 2023 | Document 36 / 48 footnote |
| **4th Extension (Current)** | **G.S.R. 214(E)** | **24th March, 2023** | **1st June, 2023** | **Document 48** |
| **5th Extension** | G.S.R. 416(E) | 5th June, 2023 | 30th June, 2023 | Document 36 |
| **6th Extension** | G.S.R. 640(E) | 30th August, 2023 | 1st October, 2023 | Document 44 |
| **7th Extension** | G.S.R. 720(E) | 6th October, 2023 | 1st January, 2024 | Document 31 |

### B. Legal Metrology Impact & Software Audit Logic
1. **Packaging Defect Exemption Horizon:**
   - Under Section 36 of the Legal Metrology Act, 2009, manufacturing, packing, or selling pre-packaged commodities in violation of PCR 2011 is an offense punishable by compounding fees or prosecution.
   - For commodities manufactured or packed between 1st April, 2023 and 31st May, 2023, lack of the newer G.S.R. 226(E) labelling attributes cannot be charged as a violation because G.S.R. 214(E) legally deferred the commencement date to 1st June, 2023.
2. **Automated Verification Engine Rule:**
   ```python
   def evaluate_pcr_226_compliance(packaging_date):
       # Statutory enforcement cutoff mapping
       if packaging_date < datetime.date(2024, 1, 1):
           # Active deferral periods (including 2023-04-01 to 2023-05-31 under G.S.R. 214(E))
           return "COMPLIANT_UNDER_TRANSITIONAL_DEFERRAL"
       else:
           return "STRICT_ENFORCEMENT_MANDATORY"
   ```

```text
1.  संजिप्त िाम और प्रारंभ - (1) इि जियमों का संजिप्त िाम जर्जधक माप जर्ज्ञाि (पैकि में रखी र्स्ट्तुएं) तीसरा संिोधि 
जियम, 2026 है।

2. जर्जधक माप जर्ज्ञाि (पैकि में रखी र्स्ट्तुएं) जियम, 2011 (जिसे इसमें इसके पश्चात् मूल जियम कहा गया है)  के  
जियम 4 में, स्ट्पष्टीकरण को उसके  (1) के रूप में क्रमांदकत दकया िाएगा और इस प्रकार क्रमांदकत स्ट्पष्टीकरण-1 के  पश्चात 
, जिम्नजलजखत स्ट्पष्टीकरण अंतः स्ट्थाजपत दकया िाएगा, अथावत्:   
सं.   377]

“स्ट्पष्टीकरण -2.- िंकाओं को िूर करिे के जलए, आयातकों को भारत में प्राजधकृत आर्थवक प्रचालक ठियर-2 और ठियर-3 
प्रमाजणत प्रचालकों को बंजधत भांडागार में अजिर्ायव घोषणाएं करिे की अिुज्ञा िी िाएगी:  
परन्द्तु दक प्राजधकृत आर्थवक प्रचालक ठियर-2 और ठियर-3 प्रमाजणत प्रचालकों के बंजधत भांडागार  को छोड़िे से 
पूर्व फुिकर पैकेि में इि जियमों के अधीि सभी अजिर्ायव घोषणाएं जर्जिर्िवष्ट होंगी। 
ठिप्पण: प्राजधकृत आर्थवक प्रचालक ठियर 2 और प्राजधकृत आर्थवक प्रचालक ठियर 3, प्राजधकृत आर्थवक प्रचालक हैं िो केंरीय 
अप्रत्यि कर और सीमा िुल्क बोडव द्वारा अिुपालि आयातकों और जियावतकों के जलए सीमा िुल्क जिकासी में तेिी लािे के 
जलए प्रिाजसत हैं।“।  
3. मूल जियमों के, जियम 27 में,––   
(i) उपजियम (2) में, खंड (ग) के पश्चात, जिम्नजलजखत खंड अंतः स्ट्थाजपत दकया िाएगा, अथावत्:  
“(घ) अजधजियम और तिधीि बिाए गए जियमों के अधीि उल्लंघिों के जलए उत्तरिायी कंपिी के जििेिक का िाम ।“; 
(ii) उपजियम (3) में, जिम्नजलजखत परंतुक अंतः स्ट्थाजपत दकया िाएगा, अथावत्:  
“परन्द्तु दक कंपिी या फमव दकन्द्ही भी जर्जिजष्टयों में पठरर्तवि के साथ अपिे ब्यौरो को र्ार्षवक रूप से अद्यति करेगी, 
जिसके जलए पूर्व र्षव के िौराि जर्जिर्मवत, पैक या आयात दकए िािे र्ाले उत्पािों को उिकी संख्या के साथ, पते और 
मूल िेि के साथ र्ार्षवक रूप से अद्यति करिे के जलए ऑिलाइि पोिवल पर जर्कल्प उपलब्ध कराया िाएगा ।“;  
(iii) उपजियम (4) के पश्चात, जिम्नजलजखत उपजियम अंतः स्ट्थाजपत दकया िाएगा, अथावत्: 
“(5) रजिस्ट्रीकरण प्रमाणपत्र रद्द होिे तक जर्जधमान्द्य रहेंगे।“। 
  
[फा. सं. I-19/50/2022- डब्ल्यू एंड एम]
```

```text
सा.का.नि. 418(अ).—केन्द्रीय सरकार, विधिक मापविज्ञान अधिनियम, 2009 (2010 का 1) की धारा 52 की उपधारा (2) के खंड (ञ) और (थ) के साथ पठित उपधारा (1) द्वारा प्रदत्त शक्तियों का प्रयोग करते हुए, विधिक मापविज्ञान (पैकेजों में रखी वस्तुएं) नियम, 2011 का और संशोधन करने के लिए निम्नलिखित नियम बनाती है, अर्थात् :- 

1. संक्षिप्त नाम और प्रारंभ - 
   (1) इन नियमों का संक्षिप्त नाम विधिक मापविज्ञान (पैकेजों में रखी वस्तुएं) तीसरा संशोधन नियम, 2026 है। 
   (2) ये राजपत्र में उनके प्रकाशन की तारीख को प्रवृत्त होंगे। 

2. विधिक मापविज्ञान (पैकेजों में रखी वस्तुएं) नियम, 2011 (जिसे इसमें इसके पश्चात् मूल नियम कहा गया है) के नियम 4 में, स्पष्टीकरण को उसके (1) के रूप में क्रमांकित किया जाएगा और इस प्रकार क्रमांकित स्पष्टीकरण-1 के पश्चात, निम्नलिखित स्पष्टीकरण अंतःस्थापित किया जाएगा, अर्थात्: 

“स्पष्टीकरण -2.- शंकाओं को दूर करने के लिए, आयातकों को भारत में प्राधिकृत आर्थिक प्रचालक टियर-2 और टियर-3 प्रमाणित प्रचालकों के बंधित भांडागार में अनिवार्य घोषणाएं करने की अनुज्ञा दी जाएगी: 
परन्तु कि प्राधिकृत आर्थिक प्रचालक टियर-2 और टियर-3 प्रमाणित प्रचालकों के बंधित भांडागार को छोड़ने से पूर्व खुदरा पैकेज में इन नियमों के अधीन सभी अनिवार्य घोषणाएं विनिर्दिष्ट होंगी। 
टिप्पण: प्राधिकृत आर्थिक प्रचालक टियर 2 और प्राधिकृत आर्थिक प्रचालक टियर 3, प्राधिकृत आर्थिक प्रचालक हैं जो केन्द्रीय अप्रत्यक्ष कर और सीमा शुल्क बोर्ड द्वारा अनुपालन आयातकों और निर्यातकों के लिए सीमा शुल्क निकासी में तेजी लाने के लिए प्रशासित हैं।”। 

3. मूल नियमों के, नियम 27 में,–– 
   (i) उपनियम (2) में, खंड (ग) के पश्चात, निम्नलिखित खंड अंतःस्थापित किया जाएगा, अर्थात्: 
   “(घ) अधिनियम और तदधीन बनाए गए नियमों के अधीन उल्लंघनों के लिए उत्तरदायी कंपनी के निदेशक का नाम।”; 
   (ii) उपनियम (3) में, निम्नलिखित परंतुक अंतःस्थापित किया जाएगा, अर्थात्: 
   “परन्तु कि कंपनी या फर्म किन्हीं भी विशिष्टियों में परिवर्तन के साथ अपने ब्यौरों को वार्षिक रूप से अद्यतन करेगी, जिसके लिए पूर्व वर्ष के दौरान विनिर्मित, पैक या आयात किए जाने वाले उत्पादों को उनकी संख्या के साथ, पते और मूल देश के साथ वार्षिक रूप से अद्यतन करने के लिए ऑनलाइन पोर्टल पर विकल्प उपलब्ध कराया जाएगा।”; 
   (iii) उपनियम (4) के पश्चात, निम्नलिखित उपनियम अंतःस्थापित किया जाएगा, अर्थात्: 
   “(5) रजिस्ट्रीकरण प्रमाणपत्र रद्द होने तक विधिमान्य रहेंगे।”। 

[फा. सं. I-19/50/2022-डब्ल्यू एंड एम] 
अनुपम मिश्रा, अपर सचिव 

टिप्पण: मूल नियम, भारत के राजपत्र, असाधारण, भाग-II, खंड 3, उपखंड (i) में संख्या सा.का.नि. 202 (अ), तारीख 7 मार्च, 2011 द्वारा प्रकाशित हुए थे और अंतिम बार संख्या सा.का.नि. 312 (अ), तारीख 27 अप्रैल, 2026 द्वारा संशोधित हुए थे।
```

### Part B: Official Gazette Text (English / अंग्रेजी मूल पाठ)

```text
[PART II—SEC. 3(i)] 
THE GAZETTE OF INDIA : EXTRAORDINARY 
3 
 
MINISTRY OF CONSUMER AFFAIRS, FOOD AND PUBLIC DISTRIBUTION 
(Department of Consumer Affairs) 
NOTIFICATION 
New Delhi, the 29th May, 2026 
 
G.S.R. 418(E).— In exercise of the powers conferred by sub-section (1) read with clauses (j) and (q) of sub-
section (2) of section 52 of the Legal Metrology Act, 2009 (1 of 2010), the Central Government hereby makes the 
following rules further to amend the Legal Metrology (Packaged Commodities) Rules, 2011, namely:–– 

1. Short title and commencement.–– 
   (1) These rules may be called the Legal Metrology (Packaged Commodities) Third Amendment Rules, 2026. 
   (2) They shall come into force on the date of their publication in the Official Gazette.   

2. In the Legal Metrology (Packaged Commodities) Rules, 2011 (hereinafter referred to as the principal rules), in  
rule 4, Explanation shall be numbered as (1) thereof and after Explanation-1 as so numbered, the following Explanation 
shall be inserted, namely:–– 

“Explanation-2.–– For the removal of doubts, the importers shall be allowed to make mandatory declarations at 
bonded warehouses of Authorised Economic Operators Tier-2 and Tier-3 certified operators in India: 

Provided that the retail packages shall have all mandatory declarations specified under these rules before leaving 
the bonded warehouses of Authorised Economic Operators Tier-2 and Tier-3 certified operators. 

Note: Authorised Economic Operators Tier 2 and Authorised Economic Operators Tier 3 are the Authorised 
Economic Operators administered by the Central Board of Indirect Taxes and Customs to speed up customs 
clearance for compliant importers and exporters.”. 

3. In the principal rules, in rule 27,–– 
(i) in sub-rule (2), after clause (c), the following clause shall be inserted, namely:–– 
“(d) the name of the Director of the company responsible for violations under the Act and rules made thereunder.”; 

(ii) to sub-rule (3), the following proviso shall be inserted, namely:–– 
“Provided that the company or firm shall update annually its details with change in any particulars for which an 
option on the online portal shall be made available to update annually with address, products with their number 
to be manufactured, packed or imported during the previous year and country of origin.”; 

(iii) after sub-rule (4), the following sub-rule shall be inserted, namely:–– 
“(5) The registration certificates shall remain valid until cancelled.”. 

[F. No. I-19/50/2022-W&M] 
ANUPAM MISHRA, Addl. Secy.  

Note: The principal rules were published in the Gazette of India, Extraordinary, Part II, Section 3, Sub-section (i) vide 
number G.S.R. 202 (E), dated the 7th March, 2011 and was last amended vide number G.S.R. 312(E), dated 27th 
April, 2026.
```

## 3. Statutory & Metrological Significance

### A. Major Administrative Reforms in G.S.R. 418(E)

| Rule Modified | Statutory Reform Introduced | Administrative & Commercial Rationale |
| :--- | :--- | :--- |
| **Rule 4: Explanation-2** | Importers permitted to apply mandatory declarations in bonded warehouses of **AEO Tier-2 and Tier-3** operators | **CBIC Customs Trade Facilitation:** Eliminates port congestion. Certified compliant importers can move imported consignments into secure customs bonded warehouses before affixing MRP, USP, Net Quantity, and Country of Origin stickers. |
| **Rule 4: Explanation-2 Proviso** | Mandatory declarations must be complete **before leaving the bonded warehouse** | Prevents non-compliant retail packages from entering the domestic stream of commerce, preserving consumer protection under the Legal Metrology Act. |
| **Rule 27(2)(d)** | Registration must name the **Director of the company responsible for violations** | **Corporate Accountability:** Direct link to Section 49 nomination. Pre-identifies the responsible corporate officer on the national register, removing ambiguity during regulatory compounding or prosecution. |
| **Rule 27(3) Proviso** | Mandatory **annual online portal update** of address, products, volumes, and Country of Origin | Shifts from static, one-time paper registrations to dynamic digital tracking of domestic manufacture, packaging, and import volumes across India. |
| **Rule 27(5)** | Registration certificates **remain valid until cancelled** | **Ease of Doing Business:** Completely abolishes periodic certificate expiry and renewal delays. Once issued, registration remains perpetually valid unless revoked for statutory violations. |

### B. Integration with NAWI Software System (SIH 26035)
1. **Manufacturer & Importer Registry Module:**
   - The test reporting software must cross-check the applicant's Legal Metrology Registration Certificate number. Under Rule 27(5), certificates no longer possess an expiry date; status is simply `ACTIVE` or `CANCELLED`.
2. **Corporate Officer Designation:**
   - In inspection test reports and verification certificates, the software can pull the named Director under Rule 27(2)(d) for official statutory notices and verification records.
3. **Customs Bonded Warehouse Verification Logic:**
   ```python
   def evaluate_import_declaration_location(importer_aeo_tier, location_type):
       # Rule 4 Explanation-2 compliance audit
       if location_type == "CUSTOMS_BONDED_WAREHOUSE":
           if importer_aeo_tier in ["TIER_2", "TIER_3"]:
               return "COMPLIANT_UNDER_RULE_4_EXPLANATION_2"
           else:
               return "NON_COMPLIANT_TIER_INSUFFICIENT"
       elif location_type == "CUSTOMS_PORT_OF_ENTRY":
           return "COMPLIANT_STANDARD_IMPORT"
   ```

## 3. Statutory & Metrological Significance

### A. Core Legal Mandate of G.S.R. 826(E)

| Category | Statutory Provision | Specific Mandate |
| :--- | :--- | :--- |
| **Target Commodities** | Rule 6(4A)(d) | **Soap, shampoo, tooth paste, other cosmetics and toiletries** (साबुन, शैम्पू, दंत मंजन, अन्य श्रृंगार एवं प्रसाधन सामग्रियां) |
| **Non-Vegetarian Origin Indicator** | Rule 6(4A)(d) | **Red dot** or **Brown dot** (लाल या भूरी बिंदी) |
| **Vegetarian Origin Indicator** | Rule 6(4A)(d) | **Green dot** (हरी बिंदी) |
| **Display Placement Constraint** | Rule 6(4A)(d) | **At the top of the principal display panel** (प्रधान संप्रदर्शन पैनल के सबसे ऊपर) |
| **Omission of Sub-Rule (8)** | Rule 6(8) Omitted | Simplification and streamlining of Rule 6 declarations |

### B. Integration with NAWI Software System (SIH 26035)
1. **Packaging Declaration Inspection Checklist Module:**
   - In automated pre-packaged commodity testing (where Class II NAWI balances perform gravimetric sampling to verify net content against declared quantity), the verification software must prompt inspectors for mandatory visual indicators:
     ```python
     def verify_cosmetic_toiletries_labelling(product_category, origin_type, dot_present, dot_color, dot_position):
         if product_category in ["soap", "shampoo", "toothpaste", "cosmetics", "toiletries"]:
             if not dot_present:
                 return "NON_COMPLIANT: Missing mandatory origin dot under Rule 6(4A)(d)"
             if dot_position != "TOP_OF_PRINCIPAL_DISPLAY_PANEL":
                 return "NON_COMPLIANT: Dot must be positioned at the top of the Principal Display Panel"
             if origin_type == "VEGETARIAN" and dot_color != "GREEN":
                 return "NON_COMPLIANT: Vegetarian origin requires Green dot"
             if origin_type == "NON_VEGETARIAN" and dot_color not in ["RED", "BROWN"]:
                 return "NON_COMPLIANT: Non-vegetarian origin requires Red or Brown dot"
             return "COMPLIANT_UNDER_GSR_826_E"
         return "NOT_APPLICABLE"
     ```
2. **Harmonization with Food Safety Labelling Standards:**
   - Extends the well-established vegetarian/non-vegetarian symbol framework from the Food Safety and Standards (Packaging and Labelling) Regulations to consumer personal care and cosmetic products under the Legal Metrology Act, 2009.

---

## 2. Executive Summary & Metrological Enforcement Architecture

### A. Statutory Purpose & Legislative Conflict Resolution
Prior to G.S.R. 858(E), statutory conflicts occasionally arose between the *Second Schedule of the Legal Metrology (Packaged Commodities) Rules, 2011* (which strictly dictates standardized package sizes for designated commodities such as baby food, biscuits, bread, tea, coffee, edible oils, pulses, etc.) and emergency orders issued by the Central or State Governments under the *Essential Commodities Act, 1955 (10 of 1955)* to curb hoarding, manage shortages, or stabilize inflation.

**G.S.R. 858(E) established clear statutory primacy for the Essential Commodities Act, 1955:**
1. **Standard Quantity Primacy (Rule 5(1)):** Where a Competent Authority under Act 10 of 1955 notifies a standard package weight/volume for an essential commodity, that notified quantity **prevails** over the Second Schedule of PCR 2011.
2. **Price Cap Primacy (Rule 6(1)(e)):** Where a Competent Authority under Act 10 of 1955 notifies or fixes a retail price cap for any essential commodity, that price cap legally governs, superseding unilateral manufacturer Maximum Retail Price (MRP) declarations.

### B. Direct Software Architecture Impact for SIH Problem Statement 26035
- **Pre-packaged Goods Audit Module:** Software verifying automated packaging or test reports for commodities subject to both Legal Metrology and Essential Commodities regulations must include an override logic checking whether an active EC Act 1955 notification is in force for the specific commodity code.

     THE   GAZETTE   OF  INDIA : EXTRAORDINARY

NOTIFICATION 
New Delhi, the 7th September, 2016 
G.S.R. 858(E).—In exercise of the powers conferred by sub-section (1) read with clause (j) of sub-section (2) of 
section 52 of the Legal Metrology Act, 2009 (1 of 2010), the Central Government hereby makes the following rules 
further to amend the Legal Metrology (Packaged Commodities) Rules, 2011, namely:- 
1. (1) Short title and commencement.—These rules may be called as the Legal Metrology (Packaged Commodities) 
(Amendment) Rules, 2016. 
(2) They shall come into force on the date of their publication in the Official Gazette. 
2. In the Legal Metrology (Packaged Commodities) Rules, 2011,-  
(a) in rule 5, in sub-rule (1), the following proviso shall be inserted namely.- 
“Provided that if the Competent Authority under the Essential Commodities Act, 1955 (10 of 1955) fixed and 
notified the standard quantity of any essential commodity, the standard quantity of such essential commodities 
as fixed and notified shall prevail.” 
(b) in rule 6,- 
after Proviso of Clause (e) of sub-rule (1), the following proviso shall be inserted namely:-  
“Provided further that if the retail sale price of any essential commodity is fixed and notified by the Competent 
Authority under the Essential Commodities Act, 1955 the same shall apply.”; 
[F. No. WM-9(31)/2016] 
P. V. RAMA SASTRY, Jt. Secy.

---

## 2. Executive Summary & Metrological Enforcement Architecture

### A. The Landmark Statistical Metrology Framework
G.S.R. 629(E) is among the most consequential enactments under the *Legal Metrology (Packaged Commodities) Rules, 2011*. It formally embeds rigorous statistical hypothesis testing and sampling quality assurance into Indian legal metrology enforcement:
1. **Consumer & Institutional Demarcation:**
   - Formal statutory definition of *"Consumer"* linked directly to the *Consumer Protection Act, 1986*.
   - Rigorous delineation of *"institutional consumers"* (commercial/service institutions buying directly from manufacturers for own consumption without retail resale) and *"industrial consumers"* (manufacturing entities buying raw materials/intermediates).
2. **Fifth Schedule Statistical Sampling Matrix:**
   - **Lot size 100 to 500:** Sample size $n = 50$, Correction factor $C = 0.379$, allowable packages with error between $MPE$ and $2 	imes MPE$ = $3$.
   - **Lot size 501 to 3200:** Sample size $n = 80$, Correction factor $C = 0.295$, allowable packages with error between $MPE$ and $2 	imes MPE$ = $5$.
   - **Lot size More than 3200:** Sample size $n = 125$, Correction factor $C = 0.234$, allowable packages with error between $MPE$ and $2 	imes MPE$ = $7$.
3. **Sixth Schedule Part II Mathematical Formulation:**
   - **Item 8 (Average Net Quantity):** $ar{x} = rac{\sum_{i=1}^n x_i}{n}$
   - **Item 9 (Sample Standard Deviation):** $\sigma = \sqrt{rac{\sum_{i=1}^n (x_i - ar{x})^2}{n - 1}}$
   - **Item 10 (Corrected Average Net Quantity):** $X_c = ar{x} + (\sigma 	imes C)$
   - **Compliance Rule:** The corrected average net quantity $X_c$ must equal or exceed the declared nominal quantity ($Q_n$), ensuring that batch variations do not systematically under-deliver goods to consumers.

### B. Direct Software Architecture Impact for SIH Problem Statement 26035
- **Statistical Batch Testing Engine:** Any NAWI software application evaluating pre-packaged goods or automated checkweighers must implement the exact mathematical formulation of Sixth Schedule Part II (calculating sample mean, sample standard deviation, and applying the correction factor $C$ based on lot size).
- **Three-Tier Acceptance Criteria:**
  1. Average net quantity condition: $ar{x} + (\sigma 	imes C) \ge Q_n$.
  2. Defect threshold condition: Count of packages with $MPE < 	ext{Error} \le 2 	imes MPE$ must be $\le$ Fifth Schedule limit.
  3. Zero-tolerance threshold condition: Zero packages allowed with $	ext{Error} > 2 	imes MPE$.

और उपधारा (2) ᳇ारा ᮧद᭜ त शि᭍ तयᲂ का ᮧयोग करते ᱟए िविधक माप िव᭄ान (पैकेज मᱶ रखी ᱟई व᭭ तुएं) िनयम, 2011 का और 
संशोधन करने के िलए िन᭥ निलिखत िनयम बनाती है, अथाᭅत:-

िविधक माप िव᭄ान (पैकेज मᱶ रखी ᱟई व᭭ तुएं) िनयम, 2011(िजसे इसमᱶ इसके प᭫ चात् उ᭍ त िनयम कहा गया है) के िनयम 2 
मᱶ,-  
(i) 
खंड (क) के प᭫ चात् िन᭥ निलिखत खंड अ᭠ त: ᭭ थािपत ᳰकया जाएगा, अथाᭅत:-  
(कक) “उपभो᭍ ता” का वही अथᭅ होगा जो उपभो᭍ ता संरᭃण अिधिनयम, 1986 (1986 का 68) कᳱ धारा 2 कᳱ उपधारा (1) के 
खंड (घ) मᱶ उसका है; 
(ii) 
खंड (खग) के ᭭ थान पर िन᭥ निलिखत खंड रखा जाएगा, अथाᭅत्:-  
‘(खग) “सं᭭ थागत उपभो᭍ ता” से ऐसी सं᭭ था अिभᮧेत है जो उस सं᭭ थान ᳇ारा उपयोग के िलए और न ᳰक वािणि᭔यक अथवा 
᭪ यापाᳯरक ᮧयोजनᲂ के िलए सीधे ही िविनमाᭅता से या ᳰकसी आयातकताᭅ से या थोक ᭪ यौहारी से पैकेज मᱶ रखी ऐसी व᭭ तुएं 
खरीदता है िजन पर ‘खुदरा िबᮓᳱ के िलए नहᱭ’ कᳱ घोषणा होगी’; 
(iii) 
खंड (खग) के प᭫ चात् िन᭥ निलिखत खंड अंत:᭭ थािपत ᳰकया जाएगा, अथाᭅत्:-

             [PART II—SEC. 3(i)] 
(खघ) “ई-कामसᭅ” से, िडजीटल और इलै᭍ ᮝािनक नेटवकᭅ पर िडजीटल उ᭜ पादᲂ सिहत माल और सेवाᲐ का ᮓय और िवᮓय 
अिभᮧेत है; 
(खड़) “ई-कामसᭅ अि᭭त᭜ व” से, क᭥ पनी अिधिनयम, 1956 या क᭥ पनी अिधिनयम 2013 के अधीन िनगिमत ऐसी कोई क᭥ पनी या 
क᭥ पनी अिधिनयम, 2013 कᳱ धारा 2 के खंड (42) के अंतगᭅत आने  वाली कोई िवदेशी क᭥ पनी, या िवदेशी मुᮤा ᮧबंध 
अिधिनयम, 1999 (1999 का 42)  कᳱ धारा 2 के खंड (v) के उप-खंड (ii) के अंतगᭅत आने वाला, भारत मᱶ ि᭭थत कोई कायाᭅलय, 
शाखा अथवा अिभकरण अिभᮧेत है, जो भारत से बाहर रहने वाले ᳰकसी ᭪ यिᲦ के ᭭ वािम᭜ वाधीन या िनयंᮢणाधीन हो  और जो 
ई-कामसᭅ ᭪ यापार करता हो। 
(खच) “ई-कामसᭅ का बाज़ार आधाᳯरत मॉडल” से,  ᳰकसी ई-कामसᭅ अि᭭त᭜ व ᳇ारा ᮓेता और िवᮓेता के बीच सुिवधाᮧदाता के ᱨप 
मᱶ कायᭅ करने के िलए िडजीटल और इलै᭍ ᮝािनक नेटवकᭅ पर सूचना ᮧौ᳒ोिगकᳱ मंच उपल᭣ ध कराना अिभᮧेत है।  
(iv)  
खंड (ड) के ᭭ थान पर िन᭥ निलिखत खंड रखा जाएगा, अथाᭅत्:-  
‘(ड) “खुदरा िवᮓय मू᭨ य” से, ऐसी अिधकतम कᳱमत अिभᮧेत है, िजस पर पैकबंद ᱨप मᱶ व᭭ तुᲐ का  उपभो᭍ ता को िवᮓय ᳰकया 
जा सकेगा इसके अंतगᭅत सभी कर भी हᲂगे;

3.
3.
3. 
उ᭍ त िनयमᲂ के िनयम 3 के ᭭ थान पर िन᭥ निलिखत िनयम रखा जाएगा, अथाᭅत्:-  
“3 अ᭟ याय का लागू होना- इस अ᭟ याय के उपबंध िन᭥ निलिखत को लागू नहᱭ हᲂगे, - 
 25 ᳰकलोᮕाम या 25 लीटर से अिधक माᮢा मᱶ व᭭ तुᲐ के पैकेट;   
(क)  सीमे᭠ ट, उवᭅरक, और 50 ᳰकलोᮕाम से अिधक के बैगᲂ मᱶ बेचे जाने वाले कृिष फामᭅ उ᭜ पाद; और   
(ख) औ᳒ोिगक उपभो᭍ ताᲐ या सं᭭ थागत उपभो᭍ ताᲐ के िलए िविनᳶमत पैकबंद व᭭ तुएं”।   
4.  
उ᭍ त िनयमᲂ के िनयम 6 मᱶ,-  
 
(i)  
उप-िनयम (1) मᱶ-  
(क)  
खंड (क) मᱶ ᭭ प᭬ टीकरण III के ᭭ थान पर िन᭥ निलिखत ᭭ प᭬ टीकरण रखा जाएगा, अथाᭅत्,-  
“᭭ प᭬ टीकरण III – पैकबंद खा᳒ व᭭ तुᲐ के संबंध मᱶ, इस खंड के उपबंध लागू नहᱭ हᲂगे, ᳴कतु, खा᳒ सुरᭃा और मानक 
अिधिनयम, 2006 (2006 का 34) और उसके अधीन बनाए गए िनयमᲂ मᱶ िविन᳸द᭬ ट  अपेᭃाएं लागू हᲂगी”।      
(ख) 
खंड (क) के प᭫ चात्, िन᭥ निलिखत खंड अंत:᭭ थािपत ᳰकया जाएगा, अथाᭅत्:-   
“(कक) आयाितत उ᭜ पादᲂ कᳱ दशा मᱶ पैकेज पर उ᳄म देश या िविनमाᭅता या जोड़ने वाले देश के नाम का उ᭨ लेख ᳰकया जाएगा”। 
(ग)  
खंड (घ) के प᭫ चात्, िन᭥ निलिखत खंड अंत:᭭ थािपत ᳰकया जाएगा, अथाᭅत्:-   
‘(घक) यᳰद ᳰकसी पैकेट मᱶ ऐसी कोई व᭭ तु है जो एक समयाविध के प᭫ चात् मानव उपभोग के िलए उपयोगी नहᱭ रह सकेगी तो 
लेबल पर “से पूवᭅ अथवा तारीख, माह, वषᭅ तक उपयोग करᱶ” का भी उ᭨ लेख होगा;’ 
परंतु इस खंड कᳱ कोई बात तक लागू नहᱭ होगी यᳰद इस संबंध मᱶ ᳰकसी अ᭠ य िविध मᱶ कोई उपबंध बनाया गया है।  
᭭ प᭬ टीकरण -1 इस उप-िनयम के ᮧयोजनᲂ के िलए, –  
(क) “से पूवᭅ” से वह तारीख अिभᮧेत है जो ᳰक᭠ ही उि᭨लिखत भंडारण पᳯरि᭭थितयᲂ के अधीन उस अविध कᳱ समाि᳙ को 
सं᭄ािपत करती है, िजनके दौरान उ᭜ पाद पूणᭅत: िवपणनयो᭏ य रहेगा और उसमᱶ वह गुणवᱫा ᮧितधाᳯरत रहेगी िजसका दावा 
ᳰकया गया है और उस तारीख के प᭫ चात्  भी वह व᭭ तु उपभोग के िलए सुरिᭃत रह सकेगी;  
(ख)  “तक उपयोग करᱶ” से वह तारीख अिभᮧेत है जो ᳰक᭠ ही उि᭨लिखत भंडारण पᳯरि᭭थितयᲂ ऐसी ᮧा᭍ कलन अविध कᳱ समाि᳙ 
को सं᭄ाि᳙ करती हᱹ, िजनके प᭫ चात् उ᭜ पाद मᱶ उपभो᭍ ता ᳇ारा सामा᭠ यतया ᮧ᭜ यािशत गुणवᱫा नहᱭ रहेगी और इस तारीख के 
प᭫ चात् वह व᭭ तु िवपणनयो᭏ य नहᱭ रहेगी;’ 
(घ)  खंड (ड़) मᱶ, ‘पैकेज के खुदरा िवᮓय मू᭨ य’ श᭣ दᲂ के ᭭ थान पर िन᭥ निलिखत रखा जाएगा, अथाᭅत्- 
“पैकेज कᳱ खुदरा िवᮓय कᳱमत मᱶ ᭭ प᭬ टत: उपदᳶशत होगा ᳰक यह सभी कर  सिहत है; और ᱨपयᲂ अथवा पैसᲂ मᱶ कᳱमत कᳱ 
गणना िनकटवतᱮ ᱨपये अथवा 50 पैसे तक कᳱ गई है

दृ᭬ टा᭠ त- खुदरा िवᮓय कᳱमत कᳱ घोषणा के िलए :

(ख) सभी करᲂ सिहत अिधकतम खुदरा कᳱमत xx.xx ᱨपये, या  
(ग) सभी करᲂ सिहत एम0आर0पी0 xx.xx ᱨपये या 
(घ) 
एम0आर0पी0 ᱨपये xx.xx (सभी करᲂ सिहत)’  
(ii) 
उप-िनयम (4) के प᭫ चात् िन᭥ निलिखत िनयम अंत:᭭ थािपत ᳰकया जाएगा, अथाᭅत्:- 
“(4क) इस िनयम कᳱ कोई भी बात, ᳰकसी िविनमाᭅता या पैककताᭅ या आयातकताᭅ को पैकेज पर अिनवायᭅ घोषणाᲐ के 
अितᳯर᭍ त, िन᭥ निलिखत कᳱ घोषणा करने के िलए िनवाᳯरत नहᱭ करेगी-  
(क) बारकोड या जीटीआईएन या ᭍ यूआर कोड;  
(ख) व᭭ तु के माᮢा आ᭫ वासन के िलए ‘ई-कोड’और अ᭠ य अपेिᭃत घोषणाएं, के᭠ ᮤीय सरकार ᳇ारा यथािविन᳸द᭬ ट रीित 
मᱶ उसे अिभᮧा᭡ त करने के प᭫ चात्; 
(ग) सरकारी ᭭कᳱमᲂ जैसे ᭭ व᭒ छ भारत िमशन, के लोगो, जहां के᭠ ᮤीय सरकार ᳇ारा इसका  उपयोग ᮧािधकृत ᳰकया 
गया हो”; 
 
(iii) 
उप-िनयम (9) के प᭫ चात्  िन᭥ निलिखत िनयम अ᭠ त:᭭ थािपत ᳰकया जाएगा, अथाᭅत्:-  
“(10) कोई ई-कामसᭅ अि᭭त᭜ व यह सुिनि᳟त करेगा ᳰक ई-कामसᭅ सं᭪ यवहार के िलए ᮧयोग ᳰकए जाने वाले िडजीटल और 
इलै᭍ ᮝािनक नेटवकᭅ पर, व᭭ तु के िविनमाᭅण अथवा पै᳴कग के मास और वषᭅ के िसवाय, उप-िनयम (1) मᱶ यथािविन᳸द᭬ ट अिनवायᭅ 
घोषणाएं कᳱ जाएं; 
पर᭠ तु ई-कामसᭅ के बाज़ार मॉडल कᳱ दशा मᱶ घोषणाᲐ के सही होने का उᱫरदािय᭜ व िविनमाᭅता या िवᮓेता या ᭪ यौहारी या 
आयातकताᭅ का होगा यᳰद,- 
(क) ई-कामसᭅ अि᭭त᭜ व के कृ᭜ य ऐसी संचार ᮧणाली तक पᱟंच उपल᭣ ध कराने तक सीिमत है िजस पर  िविनमाᭅता या िवᮓेता या 
᭪ यौहारी या आयातकताᭅ ᳇ारा जानकारी उपल᭣ ध कराई गई हो या उसे अ᭭ थाई ᱨप से भंडाᳯरत ᳰकया गया या रखा गया 
हो; या 
(ख)  अि᭭त᭜ व - 
(i) संᮧेषण आर᭥ भ नहᱭ करेगा, 
(ii) संᮧेषण के ᮧा᭡ तकताᭅ का चयन नहᱭ करेगा, और  
(iii) संᮧेषण मᱶ अ᭠ तᳶव᭬ ट जानकारी का चयन या संशोधन नहᱭ करेगा; 
(ग) अि᭭त᭜ व, सूचना ᮧौ᳒ोिगकᳱ अिधिनयम, 2000 के अधीन अपने कतᭅ᭪ य का िनवᭅहन करते समय स᭥ यक त᭜ परता का पालन 
करेगा और ऐसे अ᭠ य मागᭅदशᭅक िस᳍ा᭠ तᲂ का भी पालन करेगा जो के᭠ ᮤीय सरकार ᳇ारा इस िनिमᱫ िविहत ᳰकए जाएं;   
पर᭠ तु यह और ᳰक बाज़ार ई-कामसᭅ अि᭭त᭜ व के िलए कोई संरᭃण नहᱭ होगा यᳰद,-  
(क) अि᭭त᭜ व ने, िविध िवᱨ᳍ कायᭅ करने मᱶ धमकᳱ ᳇ारा या अ᭠ यथा वचनब᳍ता ᳇ारा षडयंᮢ या दु᭬ ᮧेरण ᳰकया हो या 
सहायता कᳱ हो या उकसाया हो;

4  
     THE   GAZETTE   OF  INDIA : EXTRAORDINARY 
             [PART II—SEC. 3(i)] 
(ख) वा᭭ तिवक जानकारी ᮧा᭡ त होने पर या समुिचत सरकार या उसके अिभकरण ᳇ारा यह अिधसूिचत ᳰकए जाने पर ᳰक कोई 
ऐसी जानकारी, आंकड़े अथवा संसूचना अि᭭त᭜ व ᳇ारा िनयि᭠ᮢत क᭥ ᭡ यूटर संसाधन मᱶ रखी गई है अथवा उससे जुड़ी ᱟई है 
िजनका उपयोग िविध िवᱨ᳍ कृ᭜ य के िलए ᳰकया जा रहा है और अि᭭त᭜ व,  उस संसाधन से उस सामᮕी को सा᭯ य से ᳰकसी 
ᮧकार कᳱ छेड़छाड़ ᳰकए िबना  त᭜ काल हटाने या उस तक पᱟंच को िनि᭬ᮓय करने मᱶ असफल रहा है; 
᭭ प᭬ टीकरण –शंकाᲐ को दूर करने के िलए यह ᭭ प᭬ ट ᳰकया जाता है ᳰक इस उप-िनयम के उपबंधᲂ मᱶ, उपभो᭍ ताᲐ को 
पᳯरदᱫ कᳱ जाने वाली पूवᭅ-पैकबंद व᭭ तुᲐ पर इन िनयमᲂ के अधीन अपेिᭃत घोषणाᲐ से ᳰकसी ᮧकार कᳱ छूट ᮧदान नहᱭ 
करᱶगे”।

उ᭍ त िनयमᲂ के िनयम 9 के उपिनयम (3) मᱶ िन᭥ निलिखत परंतुक अ᭠ त:᭭ थािपत ᳰकया जाएगा, अथाᭅत्:-   
 
“परंतु, यᳰद बाहरी पैकेज पर इन िनयमᲂ के अधीन अपेिᭃत सभी घोषणाएं अंतᳶव᭬ ट है तो अंदᱨनी पैकेज पर ऐसी कोई घोषणा अपेिᭃत 
नहᱭ होगी”।

उ᭍ त िनयमᲂ के िनयम 10 के उपिनयम (1) मᱶ -  
 
(i)   
पिहले परंतुक मᱶ “5 ᭍ यूिबक से.मी. या उससे कम” अंकᲂ, श᭣ दᲂ तथा अᭃरᲂ के ᭭ थान पर “10 ᭍ यूिबक सᱶटीमीटर या 
उससे कम” अंक, श᭣ द तथा अᭃर रखे जाएंगे;   
 
(ii)  
᭭ प᭬ टीकरण को ᭭ प᭬ टीकरण 1 के ᱨप मᱶ सं᭎ यांᳰकत ᳰकया जाएगा और इस ᮧकार सं᭎ यांᳰकत ᭭ प᭬ टीकरण के प᭫ चात् 
िन᭥ निलिखत ᭭ प᭬ टीकरण को ᮧित᭭ थािपत ᳰकया जाएगा, अथाᭅत्:-  
 
“᭭ प᭬ टीकरण 1 -  इस उपिनयम मᱶ ‘पूरा पता’ से ऐसा डाक पता अिभᮧेत से है िजस पर कारखाना  अवि᭭थत है या कंपनी या 
फमᭅ रिज᭭ ᮝीकृत है, तथा, ᳰकसी अ᭠ य दशा मᱶ िविनमाᭅता या पैककताᭅ या आयातकताᭅ के पᳯरसर कᳱ गली का नाम, सं᭎ या (यᳰद 
कोई है), और शहर और रा᭔ य का नाम जहां िविनमाᭅता या पैककताᭅ या आयातकताᭅ ᳇ारा ᭪ यापार ᳰकया जा रहा है और इसके 
साथ-साथ पो᭭ टल इंडे᭍ स नंबर (िपन) कोड, िजससे उपभो᭍ ता ᳇ारा िविनमाᭅता या पैककताᭅ या आयातकताᭅ कᳱ पहचान कᳱ जा 
सके और उसकᳱ अवि᭭थित का पता लगाया जा सके। ”

(i)   
उपिनयम (1) के प᭫ चात् िन᭥ निलिखत उपिनयम अ᭠ त:᭭ थािपत ᳰकया जाएगा, अथाᭅत्:-  
 
“(1क)   थोक ᭪ यौहारी को पूवᭅ-पैकबंद व᭭ तुᲐ को औ᳒ोिगक और सं᭭ थागत उपभो᭍ ताᲐ को सीधे ही बेचने कᳱ अनु᭄ा होगी”

“(2क)  जब तक ᳰकसी अ᭠ य िविध के अधीन िविन᳸द᭬ ट ᱨप से अ᭠ यथा उपबंिधत न हो कोई िविनमाᭅता या पैककताᭅ या 
आयातकताᭅ, उपभो᭍ ता संरᭃण अिधिनयम 1986 (1986 का 68) कᳱ धारा 2 कᳱ उप-धारा (1) के खंड (ग) के अधीन यथा 
पᳯरभािषत िनबᲈिधत ᭪ यापार प᳍ित या अनुिचत ᭪ यापार प᳍ित को अंगीकार करते ᱟए समान पूवᭅ पैकबंद व᭭ तुᲐ पर िभ᭠ न-
िभ᭠ न अिधकतम खुदरा कᳱमत कᳱ घोषणा नहᱭ करेगा।”

(i)  
ख᭛ ड (क) मᱶ “सांि᭎यकᳱय औसत” श᭣ दᲂ के ᭭ थान पर “सही औसत” श᭣ द रखे जाएंगे;  
 
(ii)  
खंड (ख) के ᭭ थान पर िन᭥ निलिखत खंड रखा जाएगा, अथाᭅत्:- :- 
 
“(ख)  
उन पैकेजᲂ कᳱ सं᭎ या िजनमᱶ कमी मᱶ ᮢुᳯट, पांचवी अनुसूची मᱶ दी गई सारणी के ᭭ तंभ 4 मᱶ िविन᳸द᭬ ट अिधकतम 
अनु᭄ेय ᮢुᳯट से अिधक पाई जाती है; या 
 
(ग)  
ᳰकसी पैकेज मᱶ कमी कᳱ ᮢुᳯट अिधकतम अनु᭄ेय ᮢुᳯट के दोगुने से अिधक है;  
 
(घ)  
िनदेशक, िनय᭠ ᮢक या िविधक माप िव᭄ान अिधकारी िविनमाᭅता या पैककताᭅ से लॉट मᱶ रखे गए पैकेजᲂ कᳱ शत-
ᮧितशत जांच करने कᳱ अपेᭃा करेगा और केवल ऐसे पैकेजᲂ के िवᮓय, िवतरण या िडलीवरी को ᮧािधकृत करेगा 
िजनकᳱ शु᳍ माᮢा मᱶ कमी कᳱ ᮢुᳯट अिधकतम अनु᭄ेय ᮢुᳯट से कम है और िविनमाᭅता या पैककताᭅ को शेष पैकेजᲂ के 
िवᮓय, िवतरण या िडलीवरी तभी अनु᭄ापत कᳱ जाएगी जब उ᭠ हᱶ यथाि᭭थित पुन: पैक या पुन लेबल, भी कर ᳰदया 
जाएगा िजससे अिधिनयम और इन िनयमᲂ के उपबंधᲂ का अनुपालन हो सके”।

उप-िनयम (5) का लोप ᳰकया जाएगा।   
 
(ग)

“(6) पैकेजᲂ के ᳰकसी लॉट का िवᮓय के िलए तभी अनुमोदन ᳰकया जाएगा, यᳰद इस िनयम के अधीन कᳱ गई परीᭃा  मᱶ यह 
पाया जाता है ᳰक – 
  
(क)  
ᮧितदशᭅ पैकेजᲂ कᳱ सही औसत शु᳍ माᮢा, घोिषत कᳱ गई शु᳍ माᮢा के बराबर या उससे अिधक है;  
  
(ख)  
अिधकतम अनु᭄ेय ᮢुᳯट से कमी मᱶ अिधक ᮢुᳯट को दशाᭅने वाले पैकेजᲂ कᳱ सं᭎ या पांचवᱭ अनुसूची मᱶ दी गई सारणी के 
᭭ तंभ 4 मᱶ दᳶशत सं᭎ या से अिधक नहᱭ है; 
 
(ग)  
ᳰकसी पैकेज मᱶ कमी कᳱ ᮢुᳯट अिधकतम अनु᭄ेय ᮢुᳯट के दोगुने से अिधक दᳶशत न हो; 
  
(घ)  
ᮧ᭜ येक पैकेज पर या उस पर लागाए गए लेबल पर इन िनयमᲂ के अधीन अपेिᭃत घोषणाएं होनी चािहए”।

यᳰद ᳰकसी पैकेज पर या उस पर लगाए गए लेबल पर इन िनयमᲂ के अधीन कᳱ जाने वाली घोषणाएं नहᱭ हᱹ; 
तो िनदेशक, िनय᭠ ᮢक या िविधक माप िव᭄ान अिधकारी िन᭥ निलिखत कारᭅवाई करेगा, अथाᭅत्:-  
(i) 
उसके ᳇ारा नमूने के ᱨप मᱶ िलए गए पैकेजᲂ का अिभᮕहण करेगा और जब तक उ᭠ हᱶ उपयु᭍ त ᭠ यायालय मᱶ सा᭯ य के 
ᱨप मᱶ ᮧ᭭ तुत नहᱭ ᳰकया जाता तब तक उन अिभᮕहण ᳰकए गए पैकेजᲂ को सुरिᭃत अिभरᭃा मᱶ रखने के िलए पयाᭅ᭡ त 
उपाय करेगा; 
(ii)  
अिधिनयम और इन िनयमᲂ के उपबंधᲂ के अनुसार, सा᭯ य के आधार पर उ᭨ लंघनᲂ के स᭥ ब᭠ ध मᱶ कारᭅवाई आरंभ 
करेगा।  
(2)  
अिभᮕहण ᳰकए गए पैकेजᲂ का ᭪ ययन दंड ᮧᳰᮓया संिहता, 1973 (1974का 2) के उपबंधᲂ के अनुसार ᳰकया जाएगा।

### Part B: Official Gazette Text (English / अंग्रेजी मूल पाठ - Complete Verbatim Text, Pages 9 to 14)
*(As published in the Gazette of India, Extraordinary, Part II, Section 3, Sub-section (i), No. 520, vide G.S.R. 629(E), dated 23rd June, 2017)*

--- [Page 9: Official Gazette Text (English)] ---

MINISTRY OF CONSUMER AFFAIRS, FOOD AND PUBLIC DISTRIBUTION 
(Department of Consumer Affairs) 
NOTIFICATION 
New Delhi, the 23rd June, 2017 
G.S.R. 629(E).—In exercise of the powers conferred by sub-sections (1) and (2) of section 52 of the Legal 
Metrology Act, 2009 (1 of 2010), the Central Government hereby makes the following rules further to amend the Legal 
Metrology (Packaged Commodities) Rules, 2011, namely:- 
 
1. (1) Short title and commencement.- These rules may be called as the Legal Metrology (Packaged Commodities) 
Amendment Rules, 2017. 
 
(2) They shall come into force on the 1st day of January, 2018.   
 
2. In the Legal Metrology (Packaged Commodities) Rules, 2011 (hereinafter referred to as the said rules), in rule 2,- 
 
(i) after clause (a), the following clause shall be inserted, namely:- 
 
‘(aa) “Consumer” shall have the same meaning as assigned to it in clause (d) of sub-section (1) of section 2 of the 
Consumer Protection Act, 1986 (68 of 1986);’; 
 
(ii) for clause (bc), the following clause shall be substituted, namely:- 
 
‘(bc) “institutional consumer” means the institution which buys packaged commodities bearing a declaration ‘not for 
retail sale’, directly from the manufacturer or from an importer or from wholesale dealer for use by that institution and 
not for commercial or trade purposes;’; 
 
(iii) 
after clause (bc), the following clauses shall be inserted, namely:- 
 
‘(bd) “E-commerce” means buying and selling of goods and services including digital products over digital and 
electronic network;  
 
(be) “E-commerce entity” means a company incorporated under the Companies Act, 1956 or the companies Act, 2013 or 
a foreign company covered under clause (42) of   section 2 of the Companies Act, 2013, or an office, branch or agency in 
India covered under sub-clause (ii) of clause (v) of section 2 of the foreign Exchange Management Act, 1999 (42 of 
1999) owned or controlled by a person resident outside India and conducting e-commerce business; 
 
(bf) “marketplace based model of e-commerce” means providing of an information technology platform by an e-
commerce entity on a digital and electronic network to act as a facilitator between buyer and seller;’; 
 
(iv) for clause (m), the following clause shall be substituted, namely:- 
‘(m) “retail sale price” means the maximum price at which the commodity in packaged form may be sold to the 
consumer inclusive of all taxes;’; 
3. In the said rules, for rule 3, the following rule shall be substituted, namely:- 
 
“3. Application of Chapter.- The provisions of this chapter shall not apply to- 
(a) packages of commodities containing quantity of more than 25 kilogram or 25 litre;  
(b) cement, fertilizer and agricultural farm produce sold in bags above 50 kilogram; and 
(c) packaged commodities meant for industrial consumers or institutional consumers.”. 
 
4. In the said rules, in rule 6,- 
 
(i) in sub-rule (1).- 
 
(a) in clause (a), for Explanation III, the following Explanation shall be substituted, namely:- 


--- [Page 10: Official Gazette Text (English)] ---
10  
     THE   GAZETTE   OF  INDIA : EXTRAORDINARY 
             [PART II—SEC. 3(i)] 
“Explanation III. - In respect of packages containing food articles, the provisions of this clause shall not apply, but the 
provisions of, and the requirements specified in the Food Safety and Standards Act, 2006 (34 of 2006) and the rules 
made thereunder shall apply;”; 
 
(b) after clause (a), the following clause shall be inserted, namely:- 
 
“(aa) The name of the country of origin or manufacture or assembly in case of imported products shall be mentioned on 
the package;”; 
 
(c) after clause (d), the following clause shall be inserted, namely:- 
 
‘(da)  If a package contains a commodity which may become unfit for human consumption after a period of time, the 
‘best before or use by the date, month and year’ shall also be  mentioned on the label: 
 
Provided that nothing in this clause shall apply if a provision in this regard is made in any other law. 
 
Explanation.- 1. For the purposes of this sub-rule,-  
 
(a) the expression “best before” means the date which signifies the end of the period under any stated storage conditions 
during which the product shall remain fully marketable and shall retain any specific qualities for which tacit or express 
claims have been made and beyond the date commodity may still be safe for consumption; 
 
(b) the expression “Use by Date” means the date which signifies the end of the estimated period under any stated storage 
conditions after which the product shall not have the quality attributes normally expected by the consumers and after this 
date, the commodity should not be regarded as marketable.’; 
 
(d) in clause (e), after the words “the retail sale price of the package;”, the following words and figures shall be inserted, 
namely:- 
 
“shall clearly indicate that it is the maximum retail price inclusive of all taxes and the price in rupees and paise be 
rounded off to the nearest rupee or 50 paise; 
  
Illustrations.- for declaration of retail sale price: 
 
(a) Maximum or Max. retail price Rs. or 
xx.xx  (inclusive of all taxes), or  
(b) Maximum or Max. retail price Rs. or 
 xx.xx inclusive of all taxes, or 
(c) MRP Rs. or 
xx.xx incl. of all taxes, or 
(d) MRP Rs. or 
xx.xx (incl. of all taxes):” 
 
(ii) after sub-rule (4), the following sub-rule shall be inserted, namely:- 
 
“(4A) Nothing in this rule shall preclude a manufacturer or packer or importer to declare the following on the package, in 
addition to the mandatory declarations- 
 
(a) Barcode or GTIN or QR Code; 
 
(b) ‘e-code’ for net quantity assurance of the commodity and other required declarations, after obtaining the same in the 
manner as specified by the Central Government; 
 
(c) logos of Government schemes, such as Swatch Bharat Mission, where such use is authorised by the Central 
Government.”; 
 
(iii) after sub-rule (9), the following sub-rule shall be inserted, namely:- 
 
“(10) An E-Commerce entity shall ensure that the mandatory declarations as specified in sub-rule (1), except the month 
and year in which the commodity is manufactured or packed,  shall be displayed on the digital and electronic network 
used for e-commerce transactions:   
 
Provided that in case of market place model of e-commerce, responsibility of the correctness of declarations 
shall lie with the manufacturer or seller or dealer or importer if,-  
 


--- [Page 11: Official Gazette Text (English)] ---
¹Hkkx IIµ[k.M 3(i)º 
Hkkjr dk jkti=k % vlk/kj.k 
11 
(a) the function of the e-commerce entity is limited to providing access to a communication system over which 
information made available by the manufacturer or seller or dealer or importer is transmitted or temporarily stored or 
hosted; or  
 
(b) the entity does not-  
 
(i) initiate the transmission;  
(ii) select the receiver of the transmission; and  
(iii) select or modify the information contained in the transmission;  
 
(c) the entity observes due diligence while discharging its duty as an intermediary under the Information Technology 
Act, 2000 and also observes such other guidelines as the Central Government may prescribe in this behalf:   
 
Provided further that there shall not be any protection to the market place e-commerce entity if,-  
 
(a) the entity has conspired or abetted or aided or induced, whether by threats or promise of otherwise in the commission 
of the unlawful act; 
 
(b) upon receiving actual knowledge, or on being notified by the appropriate Government or its agency that any 
information, data or communication link residing in or connected to a computer resource controlled by the entity is being 
used to commit the unlawful act, the entity fails to expeditiously remove or disable access to that material on that 
resource without vitiating the evidence in any manner.  
 
Explanation.- For the removal of doubts it is hereby clarified that the provision of this sub -rule shall not provide 
exemption from the declarations required to be made under these rules on pre-packaged commodities delivered to the 
consumers.”.    
 
5. In the said rules, in rule 7,- 
 
(i) for sub-rule (2), the following sub-rule shall  be substituted, namely:- 
 
“(2) The height of any numeral and letter in the declaration required under these rules shall be as per Table-I.”; 
(ii) for sub-rule (3) , the following sub-rule shall be substituted, namely:-  
 
“(3) The width of the letter or numeral shall not be less than one third of its height ,except in the case of numeral “1” and 
letters (i), ( I) and ( );”. 
 
(iii) For the Table-I, the following table shall be substituted, namely:-

(iv) Table II shall be omitted; 
 
(v) for sub-rules (4) and (5), the following sub-rules shall respectively, be substituted,  namely.- 
“(4) The area not including the top, bottom, flange at top and bottom of cans, and shoulders and neck of bottle and jars 
shall be determined in the following manner,  namely:-   
 
(a) 
in the case of a rectangular package, where one entire side can properly be considered to be the principal display 
panel side, the product of the height multiplied by the width of that side; 


--- [Page 12: Official Gazette Text (English)] ---
12  
     THE   GAZETTE   OF  INDIA : EXTRAORDINARY 
             [PART II—SEC. 3(i)] 
(b) in case of a cylindrical or nearly cylindrical package, 40 per cent. of the product of the height of the package 
multiplied by the circumference; 
 
(c) in case of any other shaped package , 40 per cent. of the total surface of the package, or an area considered to be a 
principal display panel of the package. 
(5) Except size of the numbers and letters for declaring net weight, retail sale price, date of expiry or best before or use 
by date (wherever and as applicable) and consumer care details, the provisions under sub-rules (1) to (4) shall not apply 
to a package if the information to be specified on such package under this rule is also required to be given by or under 
any other law for the time being in force.”. 
 
6. In the said rules, in rule 9, in sub-rule (3), the following proviso shall be inserted, namely,- 
“Provided that no such declarations on the inner package is required, if the outer package contains all declarations 
required under these rules.”.  
 
7. In the said rules, in rule 10, in the sub-rule 1,-   
(i) in the first proviso for the figures, words and letters “5 cubic cm or less”, the figures, words and letters “10 cubic cm 
or less” shall be substituted; 
(ii) the explanation shall be numbered as Explanation 1 thereof, and after the Explanation, as so numbered the following 
Explanation shall be substituted, namely:- 
“Explanation 1.- In this sub-rule, 'complete address' means, the postal address at which the factory is situated or company 
or firm is registered, and, in any other case, the name of the street, number (if any) assigned to the premises of the 
manufacturer or packer or importer and the name of the city and State where the business is carried on by the 
manufacturer or packer or importer and the Postal Index Number [PIN] Code so that a consumer can identify and locate 
the manufacturer or packer or importer, as the case may be.”. 
8. In the said rules, in rule 18,- 
 
(i) after sub-rule (1), the following sub-rule shall be inserted, namely:- 
 
“(1A) The wholesale dealer shall be allowed to sell the pre-packaged commodities directly to the industrial and 
institutional consumers.”. 
 
(i) after sub-rule (2), the following sub-rule shall be inserted, namely:- 
 
“(2A) Unless otherwise specifically provided under any other law, no manufacturer or packer or importer shall declare 
different maximum retail prices on an identical pre-packaged commodity  by adopting restrictive trade practices or unfair 
trade practices as defined under clause (c) of sub-section (1) of section 2 of the Consumer Protection Act, 1986 (68 of 
1986). 
 
9. In the said rules, in rule 19,- 
(a) in sub rule (4),- 
(i) in clause (a), for the words “the statistical average”, the words “the corrected average” shall be substituted; 
 
(ii) for clause (b), the following clauses shall be substituted, namely:- 
 
“(b) the number of packages that show an error in deficiency exceeding the maximum permissible error is greater than 
that shown in column 4 of Table under Fifth Schedule; or 
 
(c) any such package shows an error in deficiency exceeding twice the maximum permissible error;

# DOCUMENT 58: STATUTORY ADVISORY ON VEHICLE FUEL TANK CAPACITY IN SERVICE MANUALS VS. PHYSICAL VOLUME & 15-20% SAFETY HEADSPACE (I-1/03/2023-W&M)
# ==============================================================================

## 1. Statutory Document Metadata

| Attribute | Statutory Detail |
| :--- | :--- |
| **Document Index** | Document 58 |
| **Source File** | `dataset/2023.3.6 Fuel capacity vehicle tank_1732709098.pdf` (1 page) |
| **Document Type** | Official Statutory Circular / Advisory Guideline (Speed Post) |
| **Statutory Reference Number** | File No. `I-1/03/2023-W&M` |
| **Issuing Authority** | Government of India, Ministry of Consumer Affairs, Food and Public Distribution, Department of Consumer Affairs, Legal Metrology Division |
| **Issuing Location** | Krishi Bhawan, New Delhi-110001 |
| **Date of Issuance** | 06.03.2023 (6th March, 2023) |
| **Target Recipients** | The Controller of Legal Metrology, All States / Union Territories (UTs) |
| **Authorized Signatory** | Ashutosh Agarwal, Deputy Director (Legal Metrology) & I/c Director, Legal Metrology |
| **Signatory Contact** | Phone: 011-23389489 | Email: `ashutosh.agarwal13@nic.in` |
| **Information Copies Sent To** | All Stakeholders, Automobile Industries, Society of Indian Automobile Manufacturers (SIAM), etc. |
| **Subject Heading** | Fuel Capacity of Car / Two Wheeler's mentioned in the Service Manuals by Vehicle Manufacturers -reg. |
| **Key Regulatory Scope** | Delineation between nominal usable fuel tank capacity declared in owner manuals and the actual physical tank capacity featuring a 15% to 20% expansion buffer. |

---

## 2. Executive Summary & Metrological Enforcement Architecture

### A. The Consumer Dispute & Petrol Pump Metrological Context
A persistent source of consumer disputes and unwarranted enforcement complaints across India occurs at retail petroleum dispensing units:
1. **The Dispute Trigger:** Motorists having their two-wheelers or passenger vehicles fueled to the brim observe that the retail dispenser counter reads 15% to 20% higher than the fuel capacity stated in their vehicle's official user manual / service booklet (e.g., a dispenser fills 48 liters into a car whose manual states "Fuel Capacity: 40 L").
2. **False Allegations Against Verified Dispensers:** Consumers lodge cheating complaints against petroleum dealers and Legal Metrology dispensing units, presuming the flow meter is falsely calibrated or tampered.
3. **Representations by Petroleum Dealer Associations:** Dealer bodies petitioned the Department of Consumer Affairs (Legal Metrology Division) to issue statutory clarity on automotive design parameters.

### B. Automotive Engineering & Environmental Safety Headspace Rationale
The advisory codifies why automotive manufacturers intentionally design fuel tanks with a **15% to 20% spare capacity / ullage** above the declared booklet volume:
1. **Volatile Organic Compound (VOC) & Thermal Expansion Protection:** Underground fuel storage tanks at petrol pumps maintain lower temperatures than the ambient atmosphere. When colder fuel enters the vehicle's fuel tank, ambient heat causes volumetric thermal expansion. Without expansion headspace, liquid fuel forces open breather valves, venting toxic VOCs into the atmosphere.
2. **Vapor Space for Optimal Engine Performance:** Modern fuel-injection systems require an adequate vapor pocket for proper evaporative emission control (canister purge systems) and engine air-fuel stoichiometry. Insufficient headspace causes liquid fuel ingestion into vapor canisters, reducing engine performance and escalating hydrocarbon emissions.
3. **Rollover & Slant Spillage Fire Hazard:** If a tank is filled completely to the filler neck/brim without headspace, parking the vehicle at a slight gradient or slant forces raw fuel to leak from vent lines, presenting an immediate hazard of explosive fire ignition on hot exhaust piping.

### C. Statutory Directives to State Controllers
1. **Manufacturer Advisory via SIAM:** Automobile manufacturers must be sensitized to properly advise motorists in user manuals—advising users **not to fill the tank completely to the brim**, but only up to the declared nominal quantity.
2. **Field Officer Education:** State Legal Metrology field officers must be informed of this engineering reality to prevent wrongful penal action or registration of false metrology cases against compliant, calibrated retail dispensing units.

### D. Direct Software Architecture Impact for SIH Problem Statement 26035
- **Automated Citizen Grievance Triage Module:** When a citizen submits a short-delivery complaint on the e-Metrology / NAWI consumer grievance portal against a retail fuel dispenser, the AI engine scans the complaint for phrases like *"tank capacity was 40L but machine gave 46L"*.
- **Pre-Emptive Legal Educational Response:** The system automatically cross-references Advisory `I-1/03/2023-W&M`, provides the 15-20% thermal headspace explanation, and requests dispenser verification seal details rather than lodging a frivolous violation against verified dispensers.

---

```text
To,
        The Controller of Legal Metrology,
        All States / UTs

Subject: Fuel Capacity of Car / Two Wheeler's mentioned in the Service Manuals by Vehicle
Manufacturers -reg.

Sir/ Ma'am,

        The undersigned is directed to refer to the above mentioned subject and to state that
representations have been received from Petroleum Dealer Associations regarding wrong
tank capacity mentioned in the booklets of two wheelers and four wheelers, as it is shown 15
to 20% less than the actual volume.

2.      Association has informed that the Automobiles Manufacturer makes fuel tanks with 15
to 20% spare capacity for various security reasons viz:

(i)     To prevent Volatile Organic Compound from being leaked in case of volume expansion,
as underground tanks at petrol pumps have lower temperature compared to atmosphere.

(ii)    Gasoline needs space for vapor creation and if not provided than the output of engine
performance will suffer and unburnt fuel will add to more hydrocarbon pollution.

(iii)   If vehicle is filled with full capacity to the brim than the fuel if parked at a slant may
leak and may bring about fire being hazardous & explosive.

3.      Therefore, it is requested to create awareness among the automobile manufacturers
and request them in place of declaring the vehicle fuel tank capacity, advise the motor
vehicle users, not to fill the tank completely, but within mentioned declared less quantity.

4.      The field officers may be advised accordingly to ensure the above.
```

# DOCUMENT 59: STATUTORY ADVISORY ON PACKAGES OF AGRICULTURAL FARM PRODUCE UP TO 50 KG UNDER THE LEGAL METROLOGY (PACKAGED COMMODITIES) RULES, 2011 (I-10/9/2023-W&M)
# ==============================================================================

## 1. Statutory Document Metadata

| Attribute | Statutory Detail |
| :--- | :--- |
| **Document Index** | Document 59 |
| **Source File** | `dataset/2023.3.6 farm produce upto 50 kg as per PCR_1732709136.pdf` (1 page) |
| **Document Type** | Official Statutory Clarification Circular / Advisory (Speed Post) |
| **Statutory Reference Number** | File No. `I-10/9/2023-W&M` |
| **Issuing Authority** | Government of India, Ministry of Consumer Affairs, Food and Public Distribution, Department of Consumer Affairs, Legal Metrology Division |
| **Issuing Location** | Krishi Bhawan, New Delhi-110001 |
| **Date of Issuance** | 06.03.2023 (6th March, 2023) |
| **Target Recipients** | The Controller of Legal Metrology, All States / Union Territories (UTs) |
| **Authorized Signatory** | Ashutosh Agarwal, Deputy Director (Legal Metrology) & I/c Director, Legal Metrology |
| **Signatory Contact** | Phone: 011-23389489 | Email: `ashutosh.agarwal13@nic.in` |
| **Information Copies Sent To** | All Stakeholders, Industries, Industries Associations, etc. |
| **Subject Heading** | Packages of agriculture farm produce upto 50kg under the Legal Metrology (Packaged Commodities) Rules, 2011 -reg. |
| **Statutory Rule Interpreted** | Rule 3 of the Legal Metrology (Packaged Commodities) Rules, 2011 (Exemption Thresholds for Chapter II Application) |
| **Key Regulatory Scope** | Delineation of applicability of Chapter II for agricultural farm produce sold in bags up to 50 kg versus the standard 25 kg / 25 litre ceiling for general consumer commodities. |

---

## 2. Executive Summary & Metrological Enforcement Architecture

### A. Statutory Scope of Rule 3 Exemption
Rule 3 of the *Legal Metrology (Packaged Commodities) Rules, 2011* demarcates the jurisdictional boundary of Chapter II (mandatory retail declarations):
1. **General Commodities Rule [Rule 3(a)]:** Commodities packed in quantities exceeding **25 kilograms or 25 litres** are exempt from Chapter II requirements.
2. **Specific Industrial & Bulk Commodities Exception [Rule 3(b)]:** Specifically states that the exemption applies to:
   - *Cement sold in bags above 50 kilogram*
   - *Fertilizer sold in bags above 50 kilogram*
   - *Agricultural farm produce sold in bags above 50 kilogram*
3. **Institutional/Industrial Exemption [Rule 3(c)]:** Packaged commodities meant exclusively for industrial or institutional consumers.

### B. The Core Regulatory Clarification
Due to confusion in the trading and agro-processing community where some packers assumed the 25 kg threshold exempted agricultural bags between 25 kg and 50 kg, the Ministry issued this decisive statutory clarification:
1. **Coverage Up to 50 kg:** Packages of agricultural farm produce up to 50 kg are unequivocally **covered** under the Legal Metrology (Packaged Commodities) Rules, 2011.
2. **Freedom of Pack Sizes:** Manufacturers, packers, and importers are legally permitted to pack agricultural farm produce in **any quantity up to 50 kg** (e.g., 26 kg, 30 kg, 35 kg, 40 kg, 45 kg, 50 kg), subject to complying with all mandatory declarations under the Rules (net quantity, MRP, name/address of packer, date of packing, consumer care details).
3. **Protection of Farmers & Traders:** Field officers are instructed not to interfere with compliant packaging up to 50 kg, while ensuring mandatory consumer protections are maintained.

### C. Direct Software Architecture Impact for SIH Problem Statement 26035
- **Automated Rule 3 Exemption Validator in NAWI / Legal Metrology Suite:**
  - Standard commodities: If $	ext{Net Quantity} > 25	ext{ kg} \implies 	ext{Exempt from Chapter II}$.
  - Agricultural farm produce, cement, fertilizer: If $	ext{Net Quantity} \le 50	ext{ kg} \implies \mathbf{MANDATORY\ PCR\ COMPLIANCE\ REQUIRED}$.
  - Only if $	ext{Net Quantity} > 50	ext{ kg} \implies 	ext{Exempt from Chapter II}$.
- This advisory prevents software engines from misclassifying agricultural produce bags (e.g. 30 kg, 40 kg grain bags) as exempt!

---

## 3. Complete Verbatim Statutory Text

*(Official Advisory issued via Speed Post by the Legal Metrology Division, Department of Consumer Affairs, Government of India)*

```text

To,

        All States / UTs

Subject: Packages of agriculture farm produce upto 50kg under the Legal Metrology
(Packaged Commodities) Rules, 2011 -reg.

Sir/ Ma'am,

        The undersigned is directed to refer to the above mentioned subject and to state that
Rule 3 of the Legal Metrology (Packaged Commodities) Rules, 2011 provides that:

"3. Application of Chapter.- The provisions of this chapter shall not apply to-
(a) packages of commodities containing quantity of more than 25 kilogram or 25 litre;
(b) cement, fertilizer and agricultural farm produce sold in bags above 50 kilogram;
and
(c) packaged commodities meant for industrial consumers or institutional consumers.".

2.      Therefore, the packages of agriculture farm produce upto 50 kg are covered under
the Legal Metrology (Packaged Commodities) Rules, 2011. The manufacturers/ packers/
importers of these packages are allowed to pack in any quantity upto 50 kg as per rules.

3.      Hence, it is requested to create awareness among manufacturers/ packers/ importers
that agriculture farm produce may be packed upto 50 kg under the Legal Metrology
(Packaged Commodities) Rules, 2011.

4.      The field officers may be advised accordingly.

                                                                        Yours faithfully,

                                                                         (Ashutosh Agarwal)
                                                      Deputy Director (Legal Metrology) &
                                                               I/c Director, Legal Metrology
                                                                          Phone 011-23389489
                                                          Email: ashutosh.agarwal13@nic.in

Copy to: All stake holders/ Industries/ Industries Associations etc. for kind information.
```
